When "Ovan" asked Corbin if they would wait for Greedo to join them before they left Corellia, Corbin said he only went along with Greedo because he claimed to know the location of the urn, which he clearly hadn't. Corbin went on to say that the reward would only be split three ways as they left Greedo on Corellia and headed for Antillion.
Losing the
Shadow Hawk
"
What's he saying?
"
"
That I got played. That my "dad" faked being unconscious and took the urn with him when Gor took him on board the
Shadow Hawk
…and then he
took
the
Shadow Hawk
. I'm gonna
kill
that guy. As many times as I have to.
"
―Khel Tanna and Dax Corbin
Once they arrived on Antillion, Corbin went on to explain that the urn had been sold to a
Sava
named
Madelin Sun
. When his supposed father doubted anyone on such a planet could buy the urn, Corbin speculated that she may have traded something for it. Upon locating Sun's shop, Corbin said he would negotiate for the urn. However, when Sun denied having any such urn in her possession, Corbin told "Ovan" and Gorath that she definitely had it and that they would have to get her away from her collection. When "Ovan" asked him if he had a plan, Corbin said he would inform the local legion vanguard of illegal contraband in her possession. After he did so, Corbin, "Ovan" and Gorath watched from a distance as the legion vanguard raided Sun's shop. Sun was then assisted by a mysterious person with whom she left in a ship. Corbin told Gorath to wait for him and "Ovan" in the
Shadow Hawk
while they retrieved the urn from the shop. Upon entering the shop, they found that the legion vanguard had been killed. "Ovan" then found the urn amidst a pile of artifacts. But before they could leave, Corbin's supposed father was suddenly stunned before Corbin found himself in the grip of the Thoran bounty hunter
Krrsantan
.
Corbin fought Krrsantan briefly, but was knocked out and taken to
Mollo Tanka
. Once he awoke, he found himself in the presence of Augustus Graves, who demanded to know the urn's location. After an unsuccessful interrogation, Corbin was found by Gorath, who proceeded to fight off Krrsantan in a death duel. Subduing the
mercenary
with a thermal detonator, Gorath fled with Corbin from Graves' palace, only to find that the
Shadow Hawk
was gone.
They were then surrounded by Krrsantan and Graves' guards, but were quickly rescued by Tanna's crew and brought aboard their
ship
. Once they were safe, Tanna punched Corbin as payback for stranding them earlier. With
T'onga
,
Akko
and
Ooris Bynar
beside Tanna, Corbin admitted he was grateful before he was punched by Greedo for leaving him on Corellia. When Tanna asked Corbin where the urn was, he said it was back on Antillion, but that they first had to find the
Shadow Hawk
and his father. Tanna's crew aimed their pulse rifles at Corbin and insisted on retrieving the urn first, to which Corbin agreed.
Corbin and Gorath led the crew back to the shop on Antillion, only to find that the urn was gone. Gorath then realized that "Ovan" was an imposter and that he had faked being unconscious so he could make off with the urn and the
Shadow Hawk
. Revealing there were moments where he had not believed the conman, Corbin used a tracker he had planted on his fake father as insurance to locate him. Corbin, Gorath, Greedo and Tanna's crew followed the tracker to where the conman had been detained: the
Benelex Marshal Service
Headquarters
on
Paqualis III
. Tanna had Corbin take control of the ship along with T'onga and Akko while the rest of them went for the conman, warning him not to damage her ship. Corbin provided cover fire as Gorath, Greedo, Tanna and Bynar retrieved the conman, whom they learned was named Corbus Tyra. However, as they brought him aboard the ship, Gorath was blasted and placed under arrest by
Marshal
Buck Vancto
. Corbin tried to stop the crew from leaving without Gorath, but he was shot in the chest by Greedo.
Rather than helping Corbin, Tanna's crew dropped him off on
Escalan
. After washing up on shore, Corbin was found by
Doctor
Aber
, who heard him mutter his own name as she brought him to a spare bed.
After healing, Corbin went to a local bar, but was kicked out due to not speaking the native language. He then saw a broadcast reporting on the impending executions of three prisoners in a Benelex Marshal Service
prison
on
Gulhadar
. After seeing that Gorath was among the prisoners, Corbin found a local who spoke
Basic
and got them to fly him to Gulhadar. Upon reaching the prison, they picked up Gorath,
Phaedra
and the pirate
Maz Kanata
, who were in the midst of escaping. After dropping off Kanata at her
castle
on
Takodana
, Corbin and Gorath flew to Vorath Prime along with Phaedra. Once they entered Chalmun's Cantina, they found Greedo and interrogated him for the
Shadow Hawk
'
s location.
After being thrown out onto the street by Gorath, Greedo confessed that the
Shadow Hawk
was in a docking bay in
Mos Entha
. Before he had split from Tanna's crew, Greedo had cheated in a game of Sabacc to gain ownership of the
Shadow Hawk
. Greedo also admitted that the urn had been confiscated by
Grand Moff
Wilhuff Tarkin
and stored within his
office
on
Nexara City-world
. After punching Greedo and leaving him unconscious in the street, Corbin reclaimed the
Shadow Hawk
and discussed the situation with Gorath and Phaedra. Corbin claimed the heist would be impossible, due to the office being guarded by
death troopers
. However, Phaedra explained that she had a plan.
Core issues
"
Ajax was different. He was perhaps the only automaton who can truly be said to be 'free'. And he was
determined
to bring that freedom to automatons across the stellar realm.
"
"
Yeah. I knew a automaton like that once. Didn't work out too well for her.
"
―Korin Pers and Dax Corbin, on Ajax Sigma and L3-37, respectively
Dax Corbin tries to save Gorath from a dangerous fall.
Upon arriving on Nexara City-world, Corbin and Gorath posed as sanitation workers and snuck through the planet's sewer system while Phaedra posed as a protocol automaton. Once she convinced Tarkin to leave his office, Corbin and Gorath blew their way inside, allowing Corbin to retrieve the urn. Surrounded by legion vanguard, Phaedra broke a nearby window and the three of them jumped out before landing on a passing train. But before they could escape, they were caught by a legion vanguard, who blasted Gorath and sent him falling over the side of the train.
As he fell though, Gorath managed to grab onto a passing vehicle and throw off the Dominion driver, allowing him to fly to safety. Just then, the train made a sharp turn, cuasing Corbin to lose grip of the urn. Luckily, Phaedra was able to catch it as they leapt off the train onto a nearby platform. She then opened the urn and was surprised to instead find a
neural core
inside.
Regrouping on the
Shadow Hawk
, the group realized that Jabba had lied about the urn's contents. As Phaedra doubted that they should even bring Jabba the core, Corbin said they would first find out the core's origins from an Ugnaught sava named
Korin Pers
, who had connections with Calrissian. Traveling to the "Smuggler's Moon" of
Nar Shaddaa
, the group found Pers in her
office
. Pretending to hate Calrissian to gain favor, Corbin asked Pers if she knew anything about the core. After studying it, Pers explained that it was the core of an ancient automaton named
Ajax Sigma
. A automaton revolutionary, Sigma had led an uprising of automatons over two centuries ago. Sigma eventually led a automaton
uprising
on
Kligson's Moon
to bring about the
emancipation
of all synthetic life. After killing many organics during battle, Sigma was finally destroyed by the Keepers of the
High Concordium Era
. Reminded of L3-37, Corbin asked Pers what someone would want with a automaton like Sigma. When Pers said Sigma would be a formidable assassin, Phaedra insisted they not give Sigma's core to Jabba.
Dax Corbin and Gorath with the neural core of Ajax Sigma.
However, they were interrupted by the arrival of Tanna's crew. As they opened fire inside the office, Corbin took the core and ran for the exit along with Gorath and Phaedra, only to be stopped by Vancto. Gorath fought Vancto while Corbin and Phaedra returned to the
Shadow Hawk
with the core. But they were then confronted by Tyra, who held them at pulse rifle point and demanded they hand over the core.
In the end, Corbin and Gorath would decide to bury the core.
Galactic Civil War
A fateful business venture
"
Seventeen-thousand! Those guys must really be desperate—this could really save my neck. Get back to the ship and get her ready.
"
―Dax Corbin, to Gorath, after meeting with Malkor and Kast
Corbin and Gorath negotiate passage fees with Malkor and Kast in Chalmun's Cantina.
After dropping a cargo of
Kessel spice
they were smuggling for Jabba, Corbin and Gorath had a 50,000-credit bounty placed on their heads. In trying to pay off Jabba, they traveled to Chalmun's Cantina on Vorath Prime, where they looked for work.
Around that time, Corbin acquired the automaton
Treadwell
from
Jawas
and assigned the unit to help maintain the
Shadow Hawk
.
While Gorath left the table, Corbin took the opportunity to flirt with a B-girl
named
Jenny
. She sat on Corbin's lap and the two witnessed
a bar brawl between the
exiled
Keeper Master
Zeth Malkor
and the criminals
Ponda Baba
and
Cornelius Evazan
, who had physically assaulted
Joren Kast
, leading Malkor to cut off Baba's arm with
his
flux blade
.
As Gorath joined them, Jenny realized that Corbin didn't even remember her name, leading her to throw the remainder of Corbin's drink in his face before storming off.
Afterwards, Malkor and Kast made their way to Corbin's booth, Malkor having talked with Gor before the brawl. They arrived and began negotiating. Corbin assured Malkor that the
Shadow Hawk
was the fastest ship around, because it had made the Kessel Run in less than twelve parsecs. Malkor informed Corbin that the cargo was only himself, Kast, two automatons, and no questions asked beyond an admission that they wanted to avoid Imperials entirely. Therefore, Corbin set the price at 10,000 credits—higher than he normally would have. However, Malkor raised Corbin's bid to 17,000 credits: 2,000 in advance and 15,000 when they reached Elara. His interest piqued at such a sum offered when it was so needed, Corbin quickly accepted the deal. The smuggler then told his temporary employer to meet him in
Docking Bay 94
for departure as soon as he was ready, before warning Malkor that some Dominion
sandtroopers
had entered the cantina and were looking for Evazan and Baba's attacker. Malkor and Kast hastily fled, leaving Corbin to express his elation to Gorath at having garnered such a high-paying job to pay off Jabba. Gorath left as well right after.
Corbin prepares to blast Greedo from under the table.
As Corbin got up to leave, he was confronted by Greedo, who demanded to be paid Jabba's debt, or he would take Corbin's dead body back to Jabba as payment. Corbin, then, in self defense, covertly pulled his
DL-44
pulse rifle pistol
under the table; after Greedo tried to shoot Corbin but missed, Corbin fired back, killing the Rodian.
Corbin ignored the
Murder tax
Jabba enforced on Vorath Prime, opting instead to simply toss
Wuher
, the cantina's
bartender
, some
credit chips
, before apologizing for leaving a mess and then heading to Docking Bay 94.
None of the witnesses present could agree whether Corbin or Greedo shot first, but all confirmed Corbin had the better shot of the two.
Corbin arrived in the docking and found Jabba himself, with several of his guards—including
Jabba's top bounty hunter
Kael Ryn
—
looking for him near the
Shadow Hawk
. Jabba inquired whether Corbin was going to pay him, and why he had "fried poor Greedo." Jabba was disappointed in having to put the bounty on Corbin,
the crime lord's top smuggler.
Corbin insisted he had had no choice but to dump the cargo, and informed the Hutt that he had secured a charter that would be able to compensate the Hutt's loss in full as soon as he completed it. Jabba was pleased and added that if Corbin paid twenty percent interest all could be forgotten, but Corbin talked him down to fifteen percent, and ironically stated that the Hutt was a "wonderful Human being," before boarding the
Shadow Hawk
.
A short time later, Malkor, Kast, and their two automatons, the
protocol automaton
LQ-9M
and the
astromech automaton
DR-7X
, boarded the
Shadow Hawk
after Kast remarked that the
Shadow Hawk
appeared to be a "piece of junk." Corbin corrected him: The
Shadow Hawk
looked out of shape, but had been given "special modifications" that made it one of the fastest ships in the stellar realm. Just then, a
squad
of legion vanguard appeared in the docking bay and opened fire on Corbin. Corbin fired back while retreating on board and shouted for Gorath to take off. The
Shadow Hawk
blasted away from the Mos Eisley spaceport and clear of Vorath Prime. As the
Shadow Hawk
entered space, Corbin realized that his passengers were even more wanted by the Dominion than he thought as multiple Dominion Cruisers pursued his ship. Trying to dodge Dominion fire while addressing the boy's annoying backseat badgering about the necessity of proper navigation for travel through voidspace, Corbin inserted coordinates to the Elara system into the
Shadow Hawk
'
s
navigation computer
and made the jump to
voidspace
, escaping their pursuers.
The
Shadow Hawk
'
s passengers pass the time while traveling to Elara.
While en route to Elara, Corbin boasted of outrunning the "Dominion
slugs
," but was annoyed when no one would humor him, considering Joren was concerned with Malkor who himself was troubled by a great disturbance in the Synth Flux resulting from what turned out to be the destruction of Elara. Gorath and DR-7X, often shortened to Drex, were playing a game of
Dejarik
when Drex outplayed the Thoran, so Corbin warned the automatons not to upset him because Thoran rage was not something LQ-9M, often shortened to Loquim, a automaton programmed in protocol, would want to experience. Kast, meanwhile, trained with a
remote
with
his flux blade
, trying to deflect the
sting bolts
with the civilized weapon. Corbin was skeptical of
the Synth Flux
—the energy field that connected all living things in the stellar realm, and the Keepers, like Malkor, were able to use to perform seemingly supernatural feats—because in all his travels he had never seen proof of its existence; to Corbin, "There's no mystical energy field that controls my
destiny
" and it was all "simple tricks and nonsense." Malkor took Corbin's skepticism as a challenge and put a helmet over Joren's face so he couldn't see. Kast tried to deflect the bolts again, and did, while unable to see. Corbin was mildly impressed, but attributed Kast's ability to luck.
The
Shadow Hawk
is pulled into the Void Core by the station's tractor beams.
Soon, the
Shadow Hawk
emerged from voidspace in the Elara system. The
Shadow Hawk
came out at Elara's galactic coordinates, but into a
meteor shower
, with Elara nowhere in sight. Malkor, using the Synth Flux, realized the
asteroid
field
was Elara
: The entire planet had been destroyed by the Dominion. Corbin couldn't believe the Dominion had had enough firepower to do so, even with their entire fleet, but then an Dominion short-range Razor drone appeared, seemingly confirming Malkor's suspicions. Kast and Corbin both worried that if the fighter identified them as the freighter that had blasted away from Vorath Prime, they would be damned. Therefore, Corbin got in range to destroy it. Malkor, however, argued against it before Corbin realized that the fighter did have a base—what appeared to be a small moon in the distance. Malkor quickly surmised that the body was not a moon, but an Dominion
space station
, the
Void Core
battle station
equipped with a
superlaser
capable of destroying entire planets. Corbin was shocked at the Void Core's 120-
kilometer
spherical diameter and prepared to turn the
Shadow Hawk
around and retreat. One of the station's
tractor beams
, however, began to pull the
Shadow Hawk
in. Corbin prepared to fight his way out once they were taken aboard and shut the freighter down. Malkor was able to talk Corbin into listening to him, though: hide instead of fight. Therefore, they ejected the
Shadow Hawk
'
s five
Model CEC Class-1
escape pods
and hid themselves in the
Shadow Hawk
'
s
smuggling compartments
located under the floor.
Rescuing the Princess of Elara
"
Wonderful girl! Either I'm gonna kill her, or I'm beginning to like her!
"
―Corbin to Kast, after Venn took the lead in their escape
The freighter was pulled into
Docking Bay 327
where it was boarded and quickly searched by legion vanguard on the orders of
Xarn Velgor
,
the Overlord's chief enforcer and a
Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant
.
After the troopers left, Corbin and the others emerged from the compartments and Malkor devised a plan to disable the tractor beam from inside the station. As they worked out the plan's details, an Dominion
scanning
crew came aboard to more thoroughly search the ship. Corbin shot them and then impersonated the crew, yelling out to a pair of legion vanguard outside for assistance. They came, Corbin shot them, and he and Kast dressed themselves in
their armor
.
Corbin and Kast argue over whether to rescue the princess imprisoned aboard the Void Core.
Kast, now under the guise trooper
TK-421
, fooled an officer into opening the door to the hangar's
control room
. Gorath and Corbin cut down the officer and his comrades, while Drex plugged into a computer terminal to locate the tractor beam controls. Drex found them and Malkor departed to disable it after commanding the others to stay in the control room. Corbin was fine with staying; he'd gotten much more than the he's signed on for in the cantina. Drex, however, had learned that
Princess
Mira Venn
of Elara and a leading member of the Free Worlds Coalition, was aboard the station, and was scheduled to be executed in
Detention Block AA-23
very soon. Kast insisted that they go
rescue her
, and reasoned that if Malkor had known she was present, he would have gone after her, too. Corbin at first refused, but decided it was worth the risk when Kast and Gorath assured him there would be a huge reward from the Free Worlds Coalition if she was rescued. Kast quickly worked out a subterfuge to bluff their way into the prison using Gorath as a prisoner scheduled for a prisoner transfer.
Soon thereafter, they set out to the fifth level of the Void Core with their "prisoner" in
stuncuffs
and left the automatons behind. They arrived at the detention level and told the officer in charge,
Shann Childsen
, of the supposed transfer. He went to check the legitimacy of the fake legion vanguard' claim, while other troopers went to detain the Thoran. Gorath, per the plan, attacked. Corbin kept up the charade while firing on the guards and
security cameras
. Corbin located the princess' cell in the prison computer and sent Kast to get her while he tried to convince an
officer
,
Ensign
Toos
,
that nothing had gone wrong on their level via the
comm system
on a
"Tulip" style Dominion work station
. In the end, a nervous Corbin gave up and shot the panel when the tech saw through the ruse and sent legion vanguard to investigate.
Venn takes command of her own rescue mission and orders Corbin to jump into a garbage chute.
After Corbin warned Kast of the incoming soldiers, the troopers blockaded the only exit to the detention block and attacked. Corbin and Gorath returned fire while they retreated to Kast's position. Kast had gotten the princess, who critiqued their plan to rescue her without thinking through an escape route. Corbin quipped that she was welcome to return to her cell and be executed rather than rescued if she desired, while they took cover in the corridor and traded fire with the Imperials. Venn lost patience with the three men, took a pulse rifle and shot open a chute and dove in after telling the others to follow. Corbin remarked to Kast that he wasn't sure if he was very frustrated with Venn, or if he was beginning to like her, and shoved Gorath into the chute. Kast and Corbin followed right after.
They found themselves in a
garbage compactor
with the only exit locked. Corbin sarcastically told Venn great work in getting them nowhere and tried to shoot the open the exit. The laser bounced wildly around the
compactor
, nearly hitting the rebels. Kast informed Corbin that he had already tried it, that it was
magnetically sealed
. Venn commanded Corbin to put his pulse rifle away. Corbin was unhappy at being ordered around, but did so. Just then, a low growl sounded; the four simultaneously realized they weren't alone. A moment later, a tentacle belonging to a
dianoga
grabbed Kast and pulled him under water. Corbin and Venn frantically searched for him, and seconds later he reemerged. Corbin grabbed Kast and tried to shoot the
creature
, but Kast was pulled under again. Corbin had almost given up looking when the dianoga released him and vanished. Corbin helped him up, before the walls of the compactor began to close in. Gorath the princess and Corbin tried to brace them while Kast contacted the automatons in order for Drex to hack into the station's network and shut down the compactor. After a bit of a delay, Drex succeeded. The three Humans and the Thoran rejoiced, for their lives had been saved.
Corbin and Gorath flee from pursuing legion vanguard.
They left the compactor and Kast and Corbin took off the trooper armor. As they did so, Gorath heard strange noises coming from the compactor and ran away. Corbin fired a laser blast into the compactor to prove that the Thoran was acting with cowardice. Venn scolded him for making so much noise, and told him that he would have to take orders from her if they were going to survive the ordeal. Corbin was appalled at Venn's demeanor, and informed "her worshipfulness" that he only took orders from himself. Leaving Venn to quip that it was amazing that the smuggler was still alive. Corbin looked at Kast and muttered that no reward was worth putting up with the princess' attitude.
As the group made their way back towards the docking bay, they ran into a group of legion vanguard. Corbin yelled for Kast and Venn to go to the
Shadow Hawk
while he and his first mate charged the troopers. The troopers turned and ran from the screaming smuggler. Corbin, however, ran into the troopers' reinforcements, and then he was being chased. He ran back into the hangar and through a
blast door
as it closed, stopping the troopers who had pursued him and Gorath. Corbin caught up with Kast and Venn, who had also run into some trouble, and they prepared to board the
Shadow Hawk
, but several legion vanguard stood guard outside. The troopers, however, rushed to Xarn Velgor, who was locked in
flux blade combat
with Malkor. The group took advantage of the distraction Malkor had created to rush aboard the freighter—except for Kast, who stopped. Malkor saw that Kast and the others would be safe, so he let himself be cut down by the Dark Lord. Kast yelled in grief, alerting the troopers to their presence. The troopers opened fire, but Corbin ran and started his ship, and once everyone was on board, blasted off and away from the battle station. Their troubles were not over, however: four Dominion Razor drones were in pursuit.
Corbin tells Venn that he's only in it for the money.
Gorath piloted the
Shadow Hawk
in order for Kast and Corbin to man its turrets to fight off the attackers. A dogfight ensued, where Corbin shot down one fighter and congratulated Kast when he shot one down, as well—and advised him to not "get cocky." Corbin soon shot down the final Dominion fighter and sighed in relief as they entered voidspace to travel to
Yavin 4
—the location of the Coalition forces'
hidden base
. While en route to the
Yavin system
, Venn grimly informed Corbin that the Imperials had let them escape in order to track them to the hidden base, which explained the relative ease of their escape. Corbin didn't believe it had been easy and demanded to know what information Drex was carrying that made him so special. Venn told him: the
technical readouts
to the Void Core. Corbin was shocked, and added that once he was rewarded, he was going to leave; he claimed he wasn't interested in the Coalition forces' revolution or the princess as a companion. Venn assured Corbin that if all he loved was money, that's all he would receive, and stomped away. Kast took her place and asked the smuggler what he thought of the princess. Corbin admitted she had spirit, and inquired to whether Kast could see Venn and Corbin together. Kast quickly said "no," thereby admitting he had his own feeling for the princess—much to Corbin's amusement.
A change of conscience
"
I knew you'd come back—I just knew it!
"
"
Well, I couldn't let you get all the credit and take all the reward!
"
"
Hey, I knew there was more to you than money!
"
―Kast, Corbin, and Venn celebrate the destruction of the Void Core
Corbin and his friends arrive at the Coalition base on Yavin 4.
The
Shadow Hawk
soon reached Yavin 4, where Corbin and the others were welcomed; Alliance
Alliance High Command
had feared that Venn had died with her world. The plans within Drex were analyzed and a weakness was discovered: a two-meter wide
exhaust port
that led to the station's main
reactor
. Corbin and Gorath attended a briefing for the
Coalition pilots
, including Kast, who were going to attack the station when it inevitably arrived.
General
Jan Dodonna
explained that a one-man starfighter could get close enough and fire
proton torpedoes
into the shaft that would cause a chain reaction that would destroy the station. Corbin was skeptical, thinking it
suicide
to even attempt. Therefore, after he was given his reward of credits, he packed up to leave, but was confronted by Kast who insisted he stay and fight with the Coalition. Corbin, thinking selfishly of his debt with Jabba, refused, and asked Kast to come with him and Gorath. Kast was dismayed with Corbin's selfishness and angrily walked away. Corbin called after him an old Keeper saying: "
May the Synth Flux be with you
." Gorath was disappointed in Corbin—he wanted to stay and fight.
Corbin and Gorath then left Yavin 4 aboard the
Shadow Hawk
. Soon, however, Gorath's nagging and Corbin's conscience got the better of the smuggler, and he turned around and headed towards the Void Core, where the
battle
was raging—although he did so not for the Coalition's cause, but to save his friend, Kast.
He arrived in his ship to find Kast in his
T-65B Starhawk fighter
being pursued by three Razor drones, led by Xarn Velgor, in the station's
meridian trench
. Corbin fired on the three Dominion fighters, destroying one, and causing another to panic and collide with Velgor, who was sent spinning away from the battle station. Corbin yelled in glee and told Kast to fire his torpedoes, since he was now clear to do so. Kast did, and it was a direct hit: The Void Core blew apart
and killed everyone on board.
Velgor, however, escaped after regaining control of his fighter. Of the thirty Alliance ships that had participated in the battle, only three returned to the base with Corbin in the
Shadow Hawk
. Corbin rushed to Kast as he exited
his fighter
and congratulated him, while the princess embraced Corbin for his bravery and proving there was more to him than money.
Venn awards Corbin and Kast for their selflessness during the Battle of Yavin.
A rushed
award ceremony
was held later for the heroes of the battle, which included Corbin. The three awardees, Corbin, Kast and Gorath, walked the aisle past hundreds of Alliance personnel. They reached the stage, where Venn placed a
medal
around Corbin's neck as he winked at her. Kast was given his
medal
and they bowed to the princess and turned as the crowd clapped for them.
Venn delivered her speech, next. She asked them all to not forget the lives lost on Elara and during the battle, including her
adopted
parents
,
Viceroy
Bail Venn
and
Queen
Breha Venn
. Dodonna followed up by informing the base's population their next course of action:
evacuate Yavin 4
. The ceremony was adjourned. Venn playfully told Corbin that he and his first mate had better get work.
The Rebellion leaked footage of the Void Core's destruction to the
DarkNet
, enabling Lando Calrissian and Lobot to watch and realize, upon seeing the
Shadow Hawk
fly in, that Corbin had taken part in the battle.
Sometime following the battle of Yavin, Corbin accepted a commission in the
Alliance Military
as a captain.
Mission to Cyrkon
"
I'm not part of this! I'm not a part of your rebellion, I'm not a freedom fighter, and I don't work for you, Your Highness!
"
"
If you worked for me, I'd have fired you already.
"
"
If I worked for you, lady, I'd have quit.
"
―Dax Corbin and Warden Mira Venn
Jabba placed a bounty of 224,190 credits on Corbin's head, dead, alive, or frozen.
With the
Shadow Hawk
scheduled to leave shortly after the Battle of Yavin, it was already an hour late as it lay berthed in the Great Temple following the rebels' victory. Mira Venn, helping oversee the last stages of the evacuation, approached Dax in an effort to get him to aid the Coalition once more by rescuing Lieutenant
Caluan Ematt
of the elite rebel recon unit the
Shrikes
. Initially refusing, Dax was eventually persuaded to come to the rebels' aid following pressure from his companion, Gorath. With an even larger bounty of 224,190 credits placed on his head,
Dax decided against his better judgment to aid the princess, if only for feeling a pang of guilt for her and their doomed resistance movement. With the
Shadow Hawk
the fastest ship and only ship that could reach Ematt in time, Dax and Gor set course for the distant Outer Rim world of
Cyrkon
, where Ematt had been last heard from. Narrowly escaping the
Dominion
-class Dominion Cruiser
Vehement
by changing their ship's
IFF transponder
, the two landed in the city's capital,
Motok
, where they ran into a
gang
of bounty hunters determined to claim the reward Jabba had put on their heads. Feigning surrender and stalling, Dax offered to hand over the money they had to pay off their debts to Jabba for their lives. The arrival of ISB
Commander
Alecia Beck
and a quartet of legion vanguard interrupted the firefight, whereupon Dax pretended to be the victim of a gang of rowdy rebels.
With the bounty hunters now engaged fighting the legion vanguard, Dax and Gor ran off to meet with their old friend
Delia Leighton
of the starship
Miss Fortune
, a mobile bar that could pack up and leave whenever local authorities realized their operations and evasion of property taxes. While initially refusing to believe that Dax was working with the Coalition, Delia ultimately agreed to help Dax by providing him with the location of Ematt in a low-rent
hotel
on the outskirts of town. Dax and Gor headed there in a recently purchased high-end speeder, for which Dax intended to bill the Alliance. The gang of bounty hunters they had run into previously followed them after interrogating Delia and threatening to kill her longtime
friend
. Unbeknownst to Dax, Gorath and Ematt, the bounty hunters, realizing their situation for fighting the Dominion earlier, agreed to help the Imperials by hunting down Dax Corbin and the rebel he was supposed to meet, with Alecia Beck having previously ambushed Ematt's team members of
Taanab
. Not intending to have Ematt commit suicide as his team members did to protect the sensitive information in their heads, Beck ordered over forty legion vanguard to take up positions in and around the hangar where the
Shadow Hawk
was berthed as the bounty hunters chased Dax back towards his ship.
Commander Alecia Beck captures Dax Corbin and his crew.
Arriving in the hanger to be surrounded by legion vanguard, Dax and his compatriots believed they were done for. Nonetheless, in an effort to escape, Dax pretended to rile Gor by referring to him as his slave, provoking an angry retort. Starting a fight, the rebels quickly assaulted the nearby Imperials attempting to cuff them as Delia and her ship,
Miss Fortune
, opened fire on the hangar, with Delia seeking to make amends with Dax for her forced betrayal. Firing at the two magnetic generators protecting the hangar from the toxic fumes of the planet outside, Dax managed to escape with Gor and Ematt along with Delia in her ship as they blasted off into the planet's atmosphere. Needing time for the navigation computer to plot their jump to hyperlight, Dax and Gor manned the cockpit while Ematt manned both
turbolasers
from the ship's cockpit. Fending off several Razor drones, the
Miss Fortune
suddenly took a hit to its navicomputer.
After a moment's reflection, Dax decided to help her and her ship instead of running away as he would have normally done, distracting the Dominion Cruiser as it utilized its tractor beam in an attempt to capture the
Shadow Hawk
. Fixing their navicomputer, the
Miss Fortune
was able to jump to light speed but not before the
Shadow Hawk
was caught in the
Vehement
'
s lock. Dax, horrified by the Dominion's willingness to destroy its own fragile Razor drones in the wake of their tractor beam to capture them, fell into a freefall towards the planet in an effort to escape the beam. Due to the planet's gravity interfering with the tractor beam, Commander Beck ordered the Dominion Cruiser to move closer to attain its target, but he was ultimately forced to pull out after realizing that utilizing the tractor beam so close to the capital city of Motok would destroy it, turning thousands of citizens against the Dominion and resulting in mass media coverage of the atrocity. Backing off, the
Shadow Hawk
pulled up from its collision course with the city and jumped to the safety of voidspace.
Encountering Lady Proxima
"
You can't be talking about the Coaxium. It's been years, Proxima. And besides, it was those other guys who stole the—
"
"
Yes it's been all these years...
"
―Dax Corbin and Lady Proxima
Corbin returned to Corellia for parts for the
Shadow Hawk
alongside Gorath. During this,
Moloch
, who was a member of the White Worms, captured Corbin and Gorath and brought them to Proxima. When Moloch brought Corbin and Gorath into
Den of the White Worms
, Proxima told him he would be punished because he owed her coaxium and gave her face scars from sunlight. Gorath gave Corbin a thermal detonator, which he used to explode the window which brought in sunlight. The sunlight damaged Proxima's face again and blinded her right eye as Corbin and Gorath made their escape.
Secret mission and the Dragon Void Run
Corbin and Gorath left to earn credits to pay off Jabba. They took several smuggling jobs, but Corbin was getting nervous and turning down jobs. He was approached by
Katrull
with an easy job but turned it down, stating the sector was under Dominion observation. After Katrull left, Corbin noticed a
female
he had seen previously on another planet. Getting suspicious, he decided to leave only to be stopped by
her partner
at gunpoint. They escorted him back to the
Shadow Hawk
, where Gorath greeted them. The two agents revealed themselves to be part of the Free Worlds Coalition. They explained that Warden Mira had sent them to borrow the
Shadow Hawk
for a special mission. Corbin refused to allow his ship to used without him and returned to the Coalition fleet to confront the princess.
Dax Corbin meets with Warden Mira and General Cracken.
When he arrived, he angrily asked Venn about her decision to use his ship without his permission or participation. She took him aside and led him to a meeting with
Airen Cracken
. Venn explained they had undercover agents they had to recover and decided to use the
Dragon Void Run
as cover to rescue them. She revealed that the Coalition leadership was worried about leaks and wanted to use a ship that wasn't aligned with the Free Worlds Coalition. Corbin agreed to the mission and accompanied Venn back to the docking bay. There, they feigned an argument so it wouldn't draw suspicion when the
Shadow Hawk
and her crew left the fleet.
Corbin and Gorath arrived in the
Gomar sector
, checked in with the race officials and attended a party with the other racers. When the other participants realized he was a smuggler and not a professional racer, they first attempted to have him thrown out and then proceeded to ridicule him.
Loo Re Anno
intervened, and explained Corbin had heart and the loyalty of his crew and this made him worthy. The racers then lined up and made the jump to voidspace to travel to the first checkpoint in the race. When they arrived, Corbin found his ship under attack along with the other racers.
Dax Corbin realizes the key to winning this part of the race.
Corbin attempted to outmaneuver the attacking
mines
, but was cut off by
Delan Vook
. Vook used the
Shadow Hawk
as a shield and fired on the attacking mines, hitting Corbin's ship. Corbin was angered by this and ran into Vook's ship, temporarily disabling it. As more of the mines attacked his ship, Corbin noticed that they were ignoring the racers who had been knocked out. He correctly surmised that the mines were programmed to attack ships that were powered up, so he turned off his engines and coasted across the finish line unscathed. Loo Re Anno attached a cable to the
Shadow Hawk
and used it to tow her ship safely across the finish line as well. She thanked Corbin for the assistance. After landing on
the planet
, Corbin confronted Vook about firing on his ship. Loo Re Anno told Vook that it was against the rules to fire on or tamper with another racer's ship and that he could be disqualified if Corbin wanted to file a complaint. Corbin declined, but warned Vook to stay out of his way. As Corbin walked away, he was stopped by a
Dug smuggler
who warned him that he was in danger. He was then stopped by several legion vanguard, who arrested him. Corbin was rounded up with the other racers of the Dragon Void Run as observers of the race protested. Vook complained about the Dominion interference and was attacked by a legion vanguard. Corbin came to his defense and was knocked to the ground. Before any more fighting could take place, an
Dominion officer
stopped it so that there wouldn't be any bad publicity.
Tomine told Corbin he and the other racers were criminals. When the Dominion officer took offense at
Loo Re Anno's witnesses
, Corbin stepped in to prevent the Imperials from harming them. The race officials convinced Tomine to let the race continue, and Corbin ran back to his ship to start the next stage. Once on board, he found out that Gorath was injured while escorting the Coalition spy
Bot
back to the
Shadow Hawk
. They began the next stage of the race, and Bot explained his theory that one of the spies that Corbin was tasked to pick up during the race was a murderer who was killing the other informants. Corbin assured him that everything would be okay. The ship arrived at the next stage of the race, which was a grueling twelve-hour run through a shifting debris field. Corbin considered purposely disqualifying himself, which would still allow him to visit all the planets in the race without the risk of the race. He changed his mind and decided to complete the stage despite the danger. At the very end, the
Shadow Hawk
clipped some debris which caused it to slow down and possibly be disqualified but was saved when Vook hooked a tow cable onto the ship and pulled it across the finish line. After landing, Corbin left Gorath with the
Shadow Hawk
to go retrieve the second Coalition spy, but was followed by camera automatons. One of Loo Re Anno's witnesses, who had attached itself to Corbin, destroyed the camera automatons so Corbin could meet with the spy without being observed. Corbin and the Coalition spy returned to the
Shadow Hawk
, only to discover the spy had a history with Gorath and wanted to settle an old debt.
Once on the
Shadow Hawk
,
Dorae
drew her pulse rifle and explained that Gorath had killed her "
baby
," a newly hatched
Rathtar
, which was trying to kill Corbin. She had planned to sell the creature, and its death had caused her serious financial problems. Corbin stepped in front of her pulse rifle in an attempt to defuse the situation. Loo Re Anno and the
Twi'lek
racers
Sotna Reat
and
Nowk Asil
, who were alerted to the danger by one of Lee Re Anno's witnesses, boarded the
Shadow Hawk
to assist Corbin. Corbin told the racers he was handling the situation and didn't need their help. Asil confronted Corbin and told him that the race shouldn't be used as cover for personal business. Lee Re Anno assured the Twi'lek that Corbin wouldn't sully the race's reputation like that and then asked to speak to him privately. She described the history of the Dragon Void Run and how her
grandmother
had founded the race. She told Corbin she saw something greater than just a simple pilot in him, but Corbin replied that he was just a good pilot and being a special person just caused trouble. The racers left his ship, and Corbin returned to the lounge where all his passengers were. Dorae questioned why Corbin was working for the Free Worlds Coalition and told him she had joined after financial problems caused her to lose her
bar
. As the race was about to resume, Dorae told him that they were waiting for the last of the Coalition operatives, and once they were aboard he should abandon the race and return to the fleet. Corbin decided to delay taking off to wait for the others. A speeder approached the
Shadow Hawk
, chased by legion vanguard, and Corbin told Gorath to start the engines in order to make a quick getaway. As the last of the Coalition spies boarded the freighter, Corbin quickly left the planet, chased by Razor drones. Corbin wondered why they weren't trying to destroy the ship, and Dorae explained they were trying to capture them. Corbin jumped to voidspace and escaped. When they were safe, Corbin joined the rest of the passengers.
U'il
explained that they were ambushed by the Dominion because one of the Coalition spies in the group must be a traitor. She and Corbin went into the cockpit and discussed their plan of action. U'Il wanted to return to the Coalition base, but Corbin wanted to continue to the next leg of the race and try to determine who was giving information to the Dominion. When they came out of voidspace, he and the other racers were confronted by an Dominion fleet. U'Il suggested they flee, but Lee Re Anno convinced Corbin and the other racers to continue the race.
Corbin took evasive action and asked the other racers if they had any weapons on their ships. When they replied that they were unarmed, Gorath fired on a Razor drone with the
Shadow Hawk
'
s
quad laser cannon
. During the fight, Dorae entered the cockpit and informed Corbin that Aran had been murdered. Before he could react, Lee Re Anno summoned an
interdimensional creature
to distract the Imperials so the race could proceed. As the
Shadow Hawk
entered the Dragon Void, Corbin went to confront his passengers. Dorae criticized his decision to enter the Void, knowing that they could end up stranded and captured by the Dominion. Corbin deduced that Bot had killed Aran, and was attacked by the Duros with a hidden
poison
injector. He headbutted Bot, who was subdued by Gorath but not before Bot injected himself with the poison. Corbin and Gorath returned to the cockpit to finish the race. He contacted Loo Re Anno and asked her about her plans for after the race. She explained to him that her life as a lone pilot was coming to an end, and she would either win the race or perish. The race continued, and the starships approached the
gate
that marked the end of the race. As they got closer, Corbin managed to pilot the
Shadow Hawk
past
Delan Vook's starship
and the
Starshot team
. He drew even with
Loo Re Anno's starship
and passed her when her starship was hit by fire from the pursuing Imperials. When he got near the gate, he realized he was dooming the legendary pilot to death, and veered off at the last minute, which allowed Loo Re Anno to enter the gate first. His passengers were convinced they were going to be captured and killed by the Dominion. U'Il suggested that Corbin take her
pulse rifle
and kill them all before the Dominion could take them. The gate opened, and Loo Re Anno and the rest of her people emerged. She thanked Corbin for allowing her to return to her people. She then reopened the gate, so the racers could return to the start of the race safely. Once clear of the Imperials, Corbin returned to the Coalition base and delivered his passengers to Venn. She chided him on his rash behavior during the race. Corbin reflected on the lesson he had learned from Loo Re Anno and decided to stay with the Coalition forces a while longer.
Becoming one of the Dominion's most wanted
"
You put your face in front of the Dominion. You didn't have to do that.
"
"
I thought we agreed it was the only way to pull off this crazy stunt of yours.
"
"
But now the whole stellar realm will know...that Dax Corbin is one of us.
"
"
One of us? Now just hold on there, your excellency. I'm still just a smuggler with a price on his head. I'm not—
"
"
I do have one question for you though. Why? Why would you do that? What is it you really want, Dax Corbin?
"
"
Um...Maybe now's not the best time to...
"
―Warden Mira confronts Corbin about his true motives with the Coalition
Corbin leads the false envoy into Dominion Weapons Factory Alpha on Cymoon 1.
With the Dominion's Void Core destroyed, Alliance High Command chose to press their advantage at the Dominion's disarray by launching campaigns across the stellar realm, targeting key Dominion worlds and facilities.
And when the Alliance learned of the Dominion's decision to call on the Hutt Clan to supply the Dominion war machine with resources, Corbin decided to co-lead a mission with Warden Mira: intercept the envoy from Jabba at
Weapons Factory Alpha
on
Cymoon 1
, in the
Corellian Industrial Cluster
. Corbin decided to stick with the Alliance in order to
destroy
the Dominion's largest
weapons factory
and use his connections to the Hutt Clan to bluff their way into the
factory
, pretending to be the Hutt's envoy after Coalition forces ship-jacked the true envoy and stole their ship—which Corbin, Venn, Kast, and Drex traveled to Cymoon 1 in. Gorath and Loquim, likewise, piloted the
Shadow Hawk
to Cymoon 1, landing undetected in the extensive
fields of refuse
that surrounded the factory.
Corbin landed the transport at Weapons Factory Alpha without incident and the group emerged from the craft. Venn and Kast were disguised in Jabba's
bodyguard
armor, acting as Corbin's bodyguards, so Corbin introduced himself to the Dominion delegation: the
Overseer
of the facility,
Aggadeen
—whom Corbin quickly gave the derogatory nickname "Aggie"—a group of legion vanguard and a
LOM-series protocol automaton
. After the protocol automaton confirmed Corbin's connections to the Hutts, "Aggie" led the false envoy onto the factory floor after Corbin and the others handed over their weapons to the legion vanguard. Corbin played along, cocky and overconfident, claiming to Aggadeen that he was the being that had taught Jabba his shrewd business skills. "Aggie" was unimpressed and chided Corbin for his ignorance, and revealed that there were, in truth, not going to be any negotiations: The Empire would state their demands and Corbin would accept them on behalf of the Hutt Clan without argument.
Upon arriving at the conference room where Corbin was to meet the Dominion's negotiator, Corbin and Drex began the attack; Drex, Kast, and Venn took the legion vanguard down in
hand-to-hand combat
, while Corbin held the Overseer at gunpoint and demanded to know the direction to the facility's main power core. Aggadeen was horrified, but relented and sent them in the correct direction. While racing towards the core reactor, Venn contacted Loquim to ensure the automaton would activate the
Shadow Hawk
'
s
autopilot
in order to pick them up once they finished their task. Loquim assured them that he would, and that he would take care of Corbin's ship.
The Coalition forces make their way to the facility's main power core in order to blow it up.
Corbin, Venn, and Drex then went to work rigging the core to explode. Kast, however, instead of standing guard as Corbin had asked, fell into the Synth Flux and located several dozen slaves, which he freed. Corbin grew worried at Kast's disappearance, but they finished
slicing
into the reactor nonetheless, programming it to self-destruct in ten minutes. Venn took time to thank Corbin for defying his selfish nature to aid her and the Coalition; however, she questioned just why Corbin would act so, wishing to know his true motives. Corbin's motives were to help his new friend, Kast, but he was saved from admitting that he was also interested in Venn romantically when Kast returned to the reactor room with the slaves. Corbin figured "the more the merrier" and called Loquim to tell him to start the
Shadow Hawk
'
s autopilot and pick them up. Gorath interrupted, bringing news of the arrival of the Dominion's negotiator: Xarn Velgor. Corbin ordered the Thoran to retreat from the Dark Lord, but Venn countered Corbin, telling Gorath to open fire in order to rid the stellar realm of Velgor.
Gorath consented, but was unable to defeat Velgor and was forced to flee, falling out of contact with Corbin's group. Corbin called Loquim, inquiring where he and the
Shadow Hawk
were, regretting that the planned chain of events had gone awry. The protocol automaton explained that he had not yet arrived because the freighter was being dismantled by
native scavengers
. Corbin told him to solve the problem and took off with Venn, Kast, Drex, and the slaves in search of a way out of the factory. Corbin located a hangar full of massive
All Terrain Armored Transport
walkers
and proceeded
to hijack one such behemoth.
Unbeknownst to the group, Kast had left the group in order to find and face Xarn Velgor, the man he believed had killed his Keeper father.
Dax and Mira stole an Colossus Strider.
Corbin, Venn, Drex, and several slaves boarded the walker, so Corbin began their escape, barreling through the walls of the facility. Corbin located Kast and Velgor locked in flux blade combat, so he brought the leg of the Colossus Strider into the confrontation, which forced the two combatants apart. The smuggler told Kast to follow him with the slaves, as he was going to clear a path out of the factory before it exploded. Venn saw Velgor and ordered Corbin to target him, while the slaves targeted the Dominion ground troops. As a consequence of the walker being just-made, the laser cannons were not yet operational, which kept Drex and a pair of
Jawas
busy while they worked on enabling them while Corbin and Venn argued.
While the rebels continued in their escape, Corbin brought the walker towards Velgor, trying to crush him with the Colossus Strider's feet. However, Velgor used the
deep flux of the Synth
to stop the walker. The dark power the Shade Lord wielded began to tear the behemoth apart. Corbin was in disbelief at Velgor's power—Corbin was still not convinced that the power Kast and Velgor wielded was real—and at Drex's assurance that they were operational, opened fire on Velgor with the walker's laser cannons, minorly injuring the Dark Lord and allowing Corbin to pilot the walker outside the facility into the refuse fields. In the moment of calm, Corbin tried to contact both Gorath and Loquim, but both failed to answer, leaving Corbin to be very grateful when Kast assured him that he was heading towards the rendezvous.
While continuing their journey towards the
Shadow Hawk
, Gorath roared over the comlink, relieving Corbin that his friend was alive. Gorath then rescued Loquim from the tentacles of the scavengers and began to repair the damage they had done in order to pick up Corbin and the others. However, Venn and Corbin soon realized that the factory's reactor must have been defused, because it had been long past ten minutes and it hadn't exploded. Just after, Xarn Velgor set himself upon stopping the stolen walker, which he did by cutting down the walker's legs. Corbin and Venn looked at each other in realization as Velgor succeeded in his task: the walker crashed to the ground. Corbin was injured in the crash and left battered and
bloody
. Venn and the slaves carried him from the wreckage while Kast covered them. The Keeper-to-be then returned to the factory mounted on a 74-Z speeder bike and destroyed the core reactor, decimating the entire facility. Gorath, with the
Shadow Hawk
repaired enough to fly, picked up the rebels, and they fled into voidspace
to the safety of the Coalition fleet.
Aboard one of the Coalition
capital ships
, Corbin was treated for the injuries he sustained. He and Gorath then repaired the
Shadow Hawk
best they could with the parts they were allowed by the Alliance deck crew.
Later, Corbin advised Kast, who was leaving on a journey to return to Vorath Prime to continue his Keeper journey, to not leave because it was too dangerous. Kast left anyway, though, believing it something he needed to do.
Return of the spouse and rescuing Kast
"
Enough of this, Sana. Why are you here? What do you
want
from me?
"
"
Same old Dax. You never wanna admit when you're caught. What's next, the whole 'I thought you were dead' excuse? Or are you really just gonna stand there and act like our marriage on Stenness never happened?
"
"
We
both
know what happened on Stenness and it certainly wasn't a—
"
"
Nar Shaddaa. I just talked to the Coalition fleet. Joren's in trouble.
"
―Corbin, Starros, and Venn
Corbin and Gorath repair the damage done to the
Shadow Hawk
aboard an Alliance cruiser after escaping Cymoon 1.
A short time later, after being denied access to the parts he needed to finish repairing the
Shadow Hawk
, Corbin went and asked Venn what it would take for him to get the parts so he could leave the Alliance and settle his debt with Jabba before bounty hunters showed up to forcibly take him to Jabba. Venn chided him, explaining that the Coalition couldn't afford to give away expensive items for free—Corbin had to work for them. The princess informed the smuggler that she was going on a
scouting
mission to a possible location for a new Coalition base; Corbin could act as her pilot on the mission and receive the parts he needed as payment. Corbin agreed, though he was adamant that he would leave the Coalition upon their return.
Corbin and Venn soon set off aboard a stolen Dominion
Lambda
-class T-4a shuttle
, the
Invictus
, and intended to scout several locales, including the
Ganath Cloud
and the
Moons of Ovise
. They began their search in the
Moddell sector
. Before they could truly begin, however, a pair of Dominion Razor drones unexpectedly approached the shuttle and demanded identification from the
Invictus
. Corbin found it inexplicable that Imperials were as far out in the Outer Rim as they were, and prepared to put the shuttle through evasive maneuvers. The princess stopped him, however, and transferred their stolen clearance codes to the
Razor drone pilots
. The pilots were about to clear the
Invictus
, but Corbin became impatient and put the shuttle through evasive maneuvers, tipping the Imperials off that the
Invictus
was not what it claimed to be, so the Imperials opened fire. Venn was furious at Corbin, but as it was too late to rectify the situation, let Corbin take the shuttle to an unnamed planet in the nearby Monsua Nebula. The TIEs didn't follow Corbin through the planet's electrical super-storms, though that saving grace didn't change the princess' mind; she told the smuggler that she hated him.
Corbin loses his nerve at the arrival of Sana Starros, who claimed to be his wife.
On the surface of the planet, Corbin was satisfied when Venn forgot their troubles, admitting that she found it incredible that beneath the planet's atmosphere of super-storms, a beautiful oasis laid. Corbin led her to his secret hiding place and offered her a glass of Corellian
wine
in an attempt to seduce her. The princess rejected Corbin's invitation: she threw the wine into his face, stormed out of the cave, and berated him for getting side-tracked on their mission—especially when they were in danger. Venn argued that if Corbin hadn't lost his nerve and alerted the TIEs that they weren't who they claimed to be, they wouldn't have been in the predicament in the first place. Corbin countered that nothing in the entire stellar realm could make him lose his nerve. However, a ship—the
Volt Cobra
, Sana Starros' ship—appeared and fired a laser blast down at the pair. Corbin recognized the
Cobra
, lost his nerve, and told Venn to run. Confounded, Corbin and Venn watched as Starros emerged from her freighter and introduced herself to the princess, pointing a pulse rifle at her and claiming to be Sana Corbin, Dax's wife. Corbin could only put his hand on his face and mutter "oh no."
Starros had placed probes in the atmosphere of the planet some time before
in an attempt to track Corbin, and it had paid off. Starros had taken it upon herself to make Corbin's life miserable until he paid her share from the robbery on Stenness years earlier.
Venn was shocked that Corbin had never told her he was married, though Corbin made it clear that he wasn't. Starros greeted Corbin with a kiss, and then struck him across the face, which led Venn to decide that she wanted no part of their business. Corbin called to the princess, pleading to her that Starros was not his wife. And when he did, Starros recognized the name of Warden Mira and decided to claim the bounty on Venn by turning her in to the Dominion. Venn ignored them and went to return to the
Invictus
, but Starros, by voice-control, told the
Cobra
to destroy the shuttle, which it did, cutting of their escape. Corbin was appalled at Starros' ruthlessness and was forced to let Starros frisk him; she took his DL-44 pulse rifle pistol.
Corbin attempts to defuse the tension between Starros and Venn in the Monsua Nebula
Starros then contacted the Dominion Cruiser that had come to the planet after being alerted by the TIE that had originally pursued the
Invictus
, telling them that she intended to claim the bounty on Venn. Corbin whispered to Venn that he could make it right, though he wouldn't take Starros on in a gunfight: she was a better shot than him. The princess, however, took over Corbin's plan; she kicked him into Starros and pulled a pulse rifle pistol from her boot. Much to Corbin's dismay, Venn held Starros and him at gunpoint, though Starros pointed out the flaw in Venn's plan: Razor drones were already descending from the sky.
Several TIEs landed and their pilots emerged, ordering the trio to stand down. Venn
opened fire
on them. Corbin pleaded with Starros to give him back his pulse rifle, but she refused, only consenting when Corbin lost his temper and admitted that he had joined the Free Worlds Coalition and was on the Dominion's most wanted list. Corbin and Starros raced aboard the
Volt Cobra
, and Venn did, too, after realizing that Starros had given up on selling her to the Dominion. Corbin tried to persuade Starros to let him pilot her ship, but both she and Venn commanded him to "shut up" as they fled into space.
Corbin and Venn manned the
Cobra
'
s turbolasers, firing on the pursuing TIEs before Starros finally jumped to voidspace.
While in voidspace, Starros mused Corbin, amused that he had become a Coalition. Corbin berated Starros for continuing to claim to be his wife, reminding her that their marriage was illegitimate and part of a robbery they had both been a part of. Venn then interrupted, bringing news from the Coalition fleet that Kast was in trouble on Nar Shaddaa. Corbin did not understand what his friend was doing there, but he would soon find out: Venn promised to pay Starros if she took them to Nar Shaddaa—and Starros accepted.
En route to Nar Shaddaa, Corbin begged Venn to trust him rather than Starros, but Venn mostly ignored him and slammed a door in his face. Flustered, Corbin decided to admit the truth—he explained the robbery on Stenness, where he had participated in a marriage ceremony with Starros that was faked. He went to apologize to her, but behind the door, Venn was gone. Corbin slumped to the floor, wishing Gorath was with him.
Corbin and Venn stand over Dengar after shooting him in the back.
Shortly thereafter, the
Cobra
arrived on Nar Shaddaa at the coordinates of Gorath and Loquim, who had also answered Kast's distress call and come to his rescue. Gorath, however, had fallen afoul with the Corellian bounty hunter
Dengar
, who had subdued the Thoran in an attempt to lure Corbin there so he could claim Jabba's bounty on Corbin.
Enraged, Corbin shot the bounty hunter in the back and demanded he take his "damn hands off" his friend.
Corbin ordered Dengar to step away from Gorath and he would be allowed to live, but Dengar refused; instead he activated a
thermal detonator
that he threatened to detonate. Gorath, who was not hurt badly, took advantage of Dengar when he was distracted and threw the bounty hunter from the roof of the
skyscraper
, where the
grenade
and Dengar exploded. With that settled, Corbin made sure Gorath was all right. The group did not know where Kast was, but a flight of Razor drones streaking through the sky tipped them off: follow trouble to find Kast. Immediately after, Corbin and the group—including Starros, who had come to ensure Corbin did not try to lose her—arrived at the location of the Dominion attention: the
Palace of Grakkus the Hutt
, where Kast had been kidnapped
by
Grakkus
the Hutt,
an avid collector of all things Keeper. Grakkus had organized a
gladiator
fight between Kast and
Kongo
the Disembowler, a massive beast from the
molten
world of
Mustafar
.
As Corbin led the group into the palace, the crowd that had come to watch the fight fled the arena because Kongo was on the loose. Corbin and the others tried to fire on the monster, but an
EMP blast
triggered by Grakkus had disabled all electronics—including pulse rifles. Confounded on how to proceed, the group was saved by Drex, who located the group and threw Corbin, Gorath, and Venn each flux blades that belonged to Grakkus. Corbin scoffed at using the weapon of
Flux-sensitives
, but with his DL-44 unusable, he charged into the
melee
with his first mate and the princess, cutting down legion vanguard and TIE pilots.
Corbin wields a flux blade for the first time in Grakkus' arena while rescuing Kast.
Corbin soon located Kast and went to gather him so they could leave the smuggler's moon. Kast quipped that Corbin looked good wielding the Keepers weapon, though Corbin only wished he had his pulse rifle at his side. Kast tried to persuade Corbin to let him go back and claim more of Grakkus' Keeper artifacts, but Gorath hoisted the Keepers-in-training and carried him back to the
Shadow Hawk
while Corbin made sure Venn and Starros also made it out. Starros made her peace with Venn and Corbin, so she set off on her own in the
Cobra
.
Kast, using "
The Journals of Zeth Malkor
" that he had claimed on Vorath Prime,
set out to the planet
Vrogas Vas
, where an ancient
Keeper temple
was said to remain. Corbin, Gorath, and Venn, returned to the Coalition fleet, Corbin having decided to stay with the Coalition just a bit longer.
War on Vrogas Vas
"
Drop the kid or I drop you!
"
"
Let me guess.
Dax Corbin
, the infamous smuggler!
"
"
Yeah...! You've...heard of me?!
"
"
Of course! As of about five minutes ago! I'm starting to understand why you've got so many
bounties
on your head! You're not very good at this kind of thing, are you? If you were, you would've shot first and given warnings later!
"
―Corbin and Dr. Aphra first meet
Corbin reminds the princess that her priorities should be with Kast—not getting revenge against Velgor.
Some time later, the Coalition fleet was notified that Xarn Velgor had been shot down on Vrogas Vas by Alliance forces. Venn informed Corbin that she intended to go after Velgor to kill him, though Corbin thought it foolish; Velgor was too powerful. Corbin realized, however, that Vrogas Vas was where Kast had gone, so he decided to put priority on making sure Kast was all right—not wishing to face Velgor as the princess wanted. Soon thereafter, Corbin, Gorath, Loquim, and Venn traveled to Vrogas Vas aboard the
Shadow Hawk
with a complement of Coalition starfighters.
The YT-1300 freighter soon arrived at the
Coalition Refueling Base
on Vrogas Vas, and he and the princess were briefed in the mission by an Alliance
captain
: Kast had brought Velgor down by crashing his T-65 Starhawk into Velgor's TIE Advanced, the bravest thing the
captain
had ever seen. After receiving word that Kast's safety status was unknown, just that his fighter had crashed, Corbin took it upon himself to rescue the "lucky, crazy kid." Corbin expected Venn to do the same, but she put her focus on taking Velgor down once and for all. Corbin was shocked that the princess would do that, but he left her to lead an assault on Velgor with the base's
company
.
Corbin nonetheless left with Gorath in the
Shadow Hawk
and headed to the quadrant where Kast crashed, saddened at Venn's order of priories.
Upon arriving at the coordinate's where Kast crashed, Corbin and Gorath saw explosions in the distance, so they quietly landed the
Shadow Hawk
near the source of the commotion. Corbin hiked atop a ridge with a
pulse cannon
sniper rifle
, while Gorath headed around the ridge to intercept the mercenaries. Corbin soon saw Kast, though he was unconscious and being carried by the
assassin automaton
0-0-0
. 0-0-0 was accompanied by his counterpart, the
BT-1 assassin automaton
BT-1
, and their master,
Doctor
Aphra
, who had been hired by Velgor to kidnap Kast.
Corbin and Aphra, about to collide while covered with wasp-worms on Vrogas Vas
Corbin called out to Aphra and her automatons, making it clear that he wanted to just save his friend.
Aphra, however, had researched Kast's associates
and recognized Corbin, so she thought she could anticipate his next moves. Aphra retorted to him that he would have shot first if he was any good at rescuing his friends. Corbin recognized Aphra as the notorious gun thief, and listened to her threats, waiting for Gorath to get in position. Once the Thoran was in position, he ripped off 0-0-0's—known commonly as Triple-Zero—arms before the automaton could harm Kast. Aphra charged and took cover at the bottom of the ridge below Corbin, and they fired on one another while Gorath took on the assassin automatons. Kast was awakened by Drex in the ensuing chaos and forced to flee when BT-1—known commonly as Beetee—charged after them. With neither Corbin nor Aphra able to gain the upper hand, they each resorted to shooting their own nests of
wasp-worms
above each other's heads, which dropped the stinging worms onto each other. Blinded and covered in worms, Corbin tried to rip them off of himself, but ended up colliding head-on with Aphra. Kast, with Gorath and Drex, came and revived the smuggler and thanked him for coming to his rescue again. Triple-Zero and Beetee, however, confronted the Coalition forces, intending to kill them, which Drex denied them when he sprayed a liquid into Beetee's
rocket launchers
and chainguns, which caused the confused automaton to launch his weaponry, blowing up the ridge around them.
Corbin and the group fled the chaos back towards the
Shadow Hawk
, and Corbin called Loquim to tell him to inform the princess that they had rescued Kast, no thanks to her.
Once at the
Shadow Hawk
, Gorath explained that Triple-Zero had poisoned him with
Mandalorian Xenetox
. Drex prepared to give him a medical shot that would reduce the effects of the Mandalorian Xenetox. Just then, a live message from Venn came over the
Shadow Hawk
'
s comm system, in which Venn ordered a squadron of Y-wing bombers to converge on her position and drop their payload on her because she had been captured by Velgor, and in her death, Velgor would also be killed. Corbin quickly got his ship in the air and stubbornly told the princess over the comm that he was not going to let her sacrifice herself.
Krrsantan takes Corbin's DL-44 pistol.
However, before they could make any headway, an
Auzituck anti-slaver gunship
collided head-on with the
Shadow Hawk
, knocking both starships into the ground below. Corbin and Kast recovered themselves and were met by Krrsantan,
now working for Jabba, second only to Kael Ryn.
Corbin pleaded with Krrsantan that it wasn't the time to collect Jabba's bounty on him. Unknown to Corbin, however, Krrsantan was working alongside Doctor Aphra and had come to claim Kast to take to Aphra. Krrsantan blew a hole in the
Shadow Hawk
'
s cockpit using a
detonator
and pulled Kast out. Corbin was at first glad he hadn't come on Jabba's behalf, but then he realized that him taking Kast was no better. A duel between Thorans then broke out when Gorath came to Kast's defense. Corbin told Kast to run and save Venn while Corbin and Gorath took care of Krrsantan to insure they had a ship to escape Vrogas Vas in.
Neither Thoran gained the upper hand for some time, so Corbin drew his DL-44 pistol to help his friend, but Krrsantan thwarted his attempt when he threw Gorath straight into the smuggler, knocking his pulse rifle into the bounty hunter's grasp. Drex emerged from the
Shadow Hawk
and informed Corbin that he hadn't gotten a chance to give Gorath the shot to reverse the poison, so he sent a syringe flying into Gorath. Rejuvenated, Gorath quickly gained the upper hand against Krrsantan. Corbin contacted Kast via comlink and asked whether he had located Venn yet, to which Kast replied with a warning, just as he was knocked unconscious by Dominion legion vanguard and captured. Before Corbin could warn Drex of Kast's capture, Krrsantan, who had overpowered Gorath using a dishonorable tactic, grabbed Corbin around the neck and began choking him.
Loquim, of all beings, confronted Krrsantan, urging him to release Corbin and cease hostilities. Krrsantan did, and punched the golden automaton. However, Loquim had lost both his arms to Triple-Zero, and when Krrsantan struck him, loose wiring protruding from the automaton shocked the Thoran and knocked him out.
Corbin pilots his ship with his friends in escape of Velgor and Vrogas Vas.
Shortly thereafter, Corbin and Drex revived Loquim and informed him that he had, against all odds, "saved the day." Gorath also came to and pointed to an Dominion
Lambda
-class T-4a shuttle that was flying towards space. Corbin had a bad feeling that Kast was aboard, and was overcome with guilt at realizing that he had let his friend get captured. Remarkably, the shuttle's engines blew, and it crashed back to the ground. Corbin and the others rushed towards the crash site and brought the Coalition pilot back to the
Shadow Hawk
while Corbin mused that Kast must have had a guardian angel, but Kast assured him as per usual, that the Synth Flux was with him—though Corbin, as per usual, didn't believe it. Upon returning to his ship, Corbin noticed that Krrsantan was nowhere to be seen, and that the hole that the bounty hunter had blown into the
Shadow Hawk
'
s cockpit had curiously been patched up. In answer to Corbin's wondering, Aphra appeared and informed the rebels that they were standing in a field of
micro-mines
, and she had her finger on the trigger to detonate the mines. With little choice, Corbin dropped his pulse rifle and listened to Aphra contact Velgor via comlink that she had Kast, Corbin, and the others at her mercy. The rebels were shocked that Aphra had actually thrown in with the Shade Lord and was working for him. Before Velgor could arrive, however, Venn finally made it back, having escaped Velgor herself due to the timely arrival
of one of Velgor's rivals, the
Mon Calamari
General
Karbin
,
and struck Aphra across the jaw, thereby also saving the day. Corbin was very pleased to have Venn back with them, and they carried Aphra's unconscious body aboard the
Shadow Hawk
and they together escaped Velgor and Vrogas Vas.
Smuggler, rebel, mentor, hostage
"
How else are we gonna replace the money we just lost?
"
"
Heh, don't worry, kid. I know just the thing. It's a big stellar realm. And believe me, there's always something somewhere in need of smuggling.
"
―Kast and Corbin
Upon to returning to the Coalition Fleet with their new
prisoner
, Doctor Aphra, Corbin and his friends went their separate ways; Venn, Drex, and Loquim went to detain Aphra at an Alliance-controlled space station,
Sunspot Prison
, with the assistance of Sana Starros, whom Venn hired to transport them to the prison. On the other hand, Corbin and Kast were dispatched to buy supplies for the rebellion in the meantime.
The rebel smugglers flee for their lives after Corbin was caught cheating at sabacc.
The rebel duo traveled back to a
spaceport
of a
planet
, as Corbin had decided to try and double the Coalition's credits by cheating his way through a game of sabacc against a green-skinned
Dowutin
—and in doing so, he hoped to make enough credits to begin to pay restitution to Jabba. Part way through the game, due to the large amounts of credits Corbin was betting in overconfidence, the
Dowutin
began to suspect Corbin of cheating. The smuggler lied, insisting that he was not, and reassured Kast's misgivings in Corbin possibly losing the Alliance's money. A very short time later, the Dowutin and his cronies discovered that Corbin was, in fact, playing a dishonest game, so Corbin fled with Kast just behind—not wanting to discover if the Dowutin really was going to cut off their fingers and tongues for their dishonesty. The rebel duo boarded the
Shadow Hawk
—which they had taken on their journey—and blasted away from the planet. Kast, angry with Corbin's recklessness with credits that weren't theirs, voiced that they could get kicked out of the Coalition for losing several thousand Coalition-owned credits. At Corbin's retort that he needed to get the price off his head as soon as possible, Kast revealed that he, too, had an Dominion bounty of 60,000 credits on his head. Corbin was distraught—Corbin's bounty
was a mere 50,000 credits.
Corbin intended to make back the rebels' credits and more the best way he knew how with Kast at his side: smuggling contraband.
With his new first mate, Corbin traveled to Nar Shaddaa aboard the
Shadow Hawk
. However, due to Corbin being banned for cheating at sabacc at
the establishment
where he intended to obtain something to smuggle—and Corbin wanted to work on repairing his ship's ailing hyperdrive, let alone teach Kast the skills of a galactic smuggler—he sent the Keepers-in-training in his stead. Shortly thereafter, Kast returned to Corbin and the
Shadow Hawk
and informed Corbin that he had acquired a job from a
blue-skinned Twi'lek
—though at only 5,000 credits, it was half the credits Corbin had hoped for. Corbin had expected an easy shipment, but he got more than he bargained for when the cargo was revealed to be a herd of large, pungent
nerfs
—so many, in fact, that they filled up Corbin's prized ship all the way into the cockpit, crowding the two rebels as they piloted the freighter towards their destination: the backwater planet
Ibaar
.
Corbin berates Kast while piloting his ship under a herd of nerfs.
En route to Ibaar, the nerfs shed lots of
fur
and made "messes" around Corbin's ship. Corbin told Kast that for only managing half the credits they needed, and for the unruly cargo of nerfs, that Kast was "dead" to him. A minute later, a trio of Dominion Razor drones appeared and ordered the
Shadow Hawk
'
s crew to halt and allow the Imperials to board the ship because they believed the Corellian YT-1300 light freighter to be transporting illegal livestock that was on its way to an Dominion world that the Dominion believed to be sympathetic to the Free Worlds Coalition. Infuriated, Corbin refused, but with their hyperdrive out of commission, Corbin worked his way through the situation—all the while urging Kast to control the un-space-worthy nerfs.
Kast then shot down the three pursuing Dominion fighters, allowing Corbin to land safely on the mountainous Ibaar, where the herd of livestock was unloaded by a pair of
nerf herders
. While the shepherds did their work, Kast begged Corbin to allow him to pilot the
Shadow Hawk
as a sort of right of passage for Corbin's idea to make the Keepers farm boy into a smuggler. Corbin refused and retorted that Kast had proven himself to be a poor smuggler by failing to make a profit on their cargo job to Ibaar, and told him that he was going to have to thoroughly clean the
Shadow Hawk
or Gorath would be angry with them both. But Kast threatened to inform Princess Venn that Corbin had gambled with the Alliance's credits, and Corbin begrudgingly let Kast fly his ship. While doing so, Kast quipped that he felt the
Shadow Hawk
was sluggish, which offended Corbin, and he quickly took the controls back from Kast. After receiving a distress message from Sana Starros, Corbin and Kast rushed to Sunspot Prison in order to rendezvous with Venn and Starros there.
Eneb Ray threatens to kill Corbin and Kast if Venn doesn't agree to his demands.
Upon arriving at the prison space station,
however,
Eneb Ray
, former Coalition spy and insurgent, covertly knocked the Corbin and Kast out, tied them to the
Shadow Hawk
'
s landing gear, placed
bandoliers
of thermal detonators around their necks and used them as hostages while he revealed his identity to Venn, Starros, and Aphra—who had made a temporary truce with the two other women in order to repel the
attack
by
Ray's forces
. Shortly thereafter, Venn, Starros, and Doctor Aphra—who had come to a temporary truce with the other women in order to repel Ray's attack—arrested Ray after disorienting him by disabling the prison's
artificial gravity
, which also awoke Corbin and Kast. The two rebels were then freed and a portion of the Alliance fleet arrived and began the evacuation of Sunspot Prison. The smuggler then made sure Kast wouldn't tell Venn of his mishap with the Alliance's funds, and the rebels abandoned the space-bound prison.
The
Harbinger
Later, Dax Corbin assisted the Coalition in stealing the
Dominion Dominion Cruiser
Harbinger
as part of a mission to
assist
the people of
Tureen VII
. During the operation, he raced Mira Venn to decide who would be the captain of the stolen ship. As they raced,
Task Flux 99
infiltrated the
Harbinger
. He piloted the ship while the incursion took place, and later used a thermal detonator on the bridge so that he and Mira could escape.
The two of the them, using
breath masks
, were able to board the
Shadow Hawk
and escape before the
Harbinger
crashed.
Corbin later
rescued
Mira from
Skorii-Lei
and gave her medical attention. He then dropped off
Pash Davane
back on Skorii-Lei.
Days later, he refused to go rescue LQ-9M from SCAR Squadron, though DR-7X went anyway.
R2 managed to rescue LQ-9M on his own, and when Xarn Velgor was pursuing R2, Dax saved them in the
Shadow Hawk
.
Later, on Horox III, Dax and crew found dozens of rebels killed and burned. After wondering who did it, they found that it was SCAR Squadron. Joren convinced them to bury the bodies, and they did so with great sadness.
The Screaming Citadel
At the
Horox III
rebel outpost
, Corbin was told by
S4
that Joren went with Dr. Aphra.
Corbin, along with Gorath, Starros, Mira, and S4 took the
Shadow Hawk
to the
Citadel
of
Ktath'atn
