. Upon their arrival at Ktath'atn, Corbin and the others met 0-0-0 and BT-1.
They then met up with Kast and Aphra at the
Ark Angel II
. Sol told her that the
Ark Angel
'
s aft thruster was misaligned. He was then confronted by
Varroa
and several of the
Queen's
guards. Corbin and the others then hid behind a door. Inside the door were
Abersyn symbiotes
. Corbin attempted to confront the Queen but was hypnotized.
Corbin was taken to the queen's chamber, where one of the symbiotes stung him in the neck. He was then forced to drink something that forced him to serve the queen. Corbin was given armor and forced to track down Mira and Starros. Corbin and the guards cornered Mira. He attempted to use a symbiote on her but was stopped when Starros and Aphra's automatons arrived.
Corbin attempted to shoot Mira and Starros, but Mira was able to convince him and the guards to lower their weapons. Varroa attempted to reassert the hive's control over him, but Corbin called in Black Krrsantan. Corbin was able to take control of the other guards and killed Varroa. Corbin was named King by the guards, but did not want to tell people what to do. He was able to disband the hive and free the people. Corbin was then sedated by Triple-Zero. Corbin, Starros, Kast, and Mira left Ktath'atn knowing that it was freed.
Escorting Grakkus
"
You're risking your life to take me to jail, bu the irony is... ...You're the one who's truly in prison, Dax Corbin.
"
―Grakkus
Corbin was asked by Mothma to take Grakkus to
Akiva
for interrogation and imprisonment by the Alliance. En route to Akiva, Corbin threatened to shoot Grakkus if he tried anything against him or Gor. Grakkus tried to convince Corbin to free him and return to his life as smuggler. As Corbin thought about Grakkus' offer,
the
Shadow Hawk
came under attack by three TIEs.
Corbin realized that Grakkus had disabled the hyperdrive. Corbin attempted to evade the TIE patrol to no avail. He decided to contact the TIEs and surrender to them. Corbin landed the
Shadow Hawk
on a asteroid where the three TIE pilots boarded the ship and met Grakkus. After Grakkus killed on of the pilots, Sol finished off the last two after hiding in the
Shadow Hawk
'
s secret compartments. Corbin turned Grakkus' offer and decided to resume his voyage to Akiva. Upon hearing this, Grakkus grabbed Corbin by the neck and demanded that Gor take him to
Teth
, where his
safe house
was. Upon confirming it, Corbin shocked Grakkus and sent the information to General Draven. Corbin told Grakkus that it was all a setup to get his safe house's location. After Starhawks arrived, Corbin knocked Grakkus out and took him to prison.
Trouble on Odona
"
You left me to die for two measly tons of rock, Corbin.
"
"
And now this is how it ends for you. You die here alone.
"
"
A life worth less than a single stone.
"
―Frax to Dax Corbin
A creature attacks Dax and Mira.
Corbin took Mira to Odona to see if it was a viable location for a new rebel base. However, they got lost in a series of caves for three days and were attacked by a
creature
. Unbeknownst to Corbin or Mira, they were tracked and followed by Frax,
Selt
, and a automaton. Corbin came under fire and took Mira to cover. He then told Mira to get to the
Shadow Hawk
. Corbin returned to the caves and was confronted by Frax, who wanted revenge against Corbin for dropping him into the Cordaxian Sea to be captured by the Dominion. Corbin tried to run, but was stopped by Frax and bounty hunters. However, Mira was able to light the automaton on fire and escape Frax.
Dax and Mira make their way back to the
Shadow Hawk
.
As Corbin and Mira made their way back to the
Shadow Hawk
, they were confronted by Frax, who attempted to shoot him despite Mira's threat to shoot him. Despite missing Frax, Mira was able to cause ice to come at him and freeze him to death. Corbin and Mira were found by Gor and taken to the Coalition fleet to recover.
Fighting with the Partisans
Corbin, along with Mira and Kast, went to
Jedha
. There, they
met
and rescued
Chulco Gi
and former
Free Worlds Coalition Special Forces
soldier and
Partisan
Ubin Des
. After escaping the Dominion
Snowtrooper
Patrol, Corbin took the
Shadow Hawk
to
NaJedha
so Mira could speak with Des about a meeting with the surviving Partisans. Corbin allowed himself to be blindfolded and let Des and Gi fly the
Shadow Hawk
to the new Partisan base. Corbin and the others were brought before
Benthic
.
Corbin told Benthic to either kill him or show them the Partisans' famous decisiveness. Benthic freed them and spoke with Mira, Corbin, and Kast about destroying the
Drill citadel
.
As the partisans attacked the drill citadel. Corbin and Mira flew the
Shadow Hawk
towards the drill. Corbin was able to destroy the citadel and return to the Partisans' base.
The Empire then deployed the
Leviathan
. Corbin, Mira, and Benthic looked at the
Leviathan
and saw no explosive that could damage it. After Des' explosives failed to make a dent in the
Leviathan
, Corbin flew an
airspeeder
with Mira as his gunner. Together they rescued Des and her team from the
Leviathan
'
s tracks. Corbin chastised Des for making a suicide run and told her not to do it unless there was no other choice. Corbin was then informed Gorath was coming to help them finish off the
Leviathan
. Corbin met up with Gor at the base after Kast rescued him with information from Shu-Torun.
With the Patisans, Corbin and the others made a plan to attack the
Leviathan
using the plans Gor acquired. Corbin agreed to fly the mission and keep an eye on the partisans. As the partisans boarded the
Shadow Hawk
, Corbin and the others came under attack by legion vanguard. Benthic was wounded and Corbin told partisans to get aboard the
Shadow Hawk
to escape. As the
Shadow Hawk
made its escape, Corbin did not believe that Des should be leading the mission. Corbin was reluctant to take the lead, but was convinced by Gor to take charge since he was the
Shadow Hawk
'
s captain. Corbin saw that the
Leviathan
'
s guns were down and led the partisans in attacking the legion vanguard stationed there.
Corbin and the Partisans were able to make their way to the bridge. On the bridge, Corbin, Mira, Des, and the partisans were able to pilot the
Leviathan
into Jedha City's crater. Corbin ordered the Partisans to barricade the bridge to prevent the Dominion from stopping their plans. Corbin and the Partisans were able to return to the
Shadow Hawk
before it fell into the crater. Corbin took the partisans back to NaJedha. Benthic told Corbin that he and his fellow partisans would guard Jedha's ashes until it was gone. Before leaving, Corbin awarded Des the Medal of Bravery for her actions, even though it was a copy.
Crait
From the captain's seat of the
Shadow Hawk
, Corbin led the defense of the Coalition fleet from an
attack
by Dominion forces, buying time for the fleet to jump to
Baraan-Fa
. Shortly after, he accompanied
Wedge Antilles
, Mira, Joren, and Gorath on a
mission to Crait
, which the Alliance was considering as a location for its hidden base of operations. There they met
Trusk Berinato
, an old ally of Bail Venn who Mira thought would help the Alliance. Instead Trusk had forged environmental reports and summoned SCAR Squadron to eliminate the Coalition forces. Corbin attempted to evacuate Mira while Joren and Wedge engaged SCAR Squadron, but Trusk shot him in the shoulder. Mira returned fire, incapacitating Trusk as the Coalition forces regrouped and left the system aboard the
Shadow Hawk
.
Mutiny on Mon Cala
Corbin took Admiral
Gial Ackbar
, Kast, and Mira to Mon Cala. There, they met with Mon Cala's
Regent
,
Grand Admiral
Dors Urtya
. Ackbar and Mira asked Urtya for the
Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet
to become part of the Free Worlds Coalition's fleet. Corbin spotted a
patrol transport
approaching their position and came under attack. The transport landed, and Corbin approached the troopers. Corbin was able to bribe the sergeant and toss a detonator into the transport. Corbin called to Gor to bring up the boat so they could escape. Corbin drove the boat past several creatures. Unfortunately, Urtya refused to give the Alliance the fleet, and Corbin took Mira and Ackbar back to the
Mako-Ta Space Docks
. There, he gave Kast a drink.
In the mess hall, they met up with Wedge Antilles,
"Hobbie" Klivian
, and
Zev Senesca
. Together, they commemorated the fallen pilots of
Red Squadron
.
Mira arrived in the hall with a mission. With Kast and Gor, Corbin went with Mira to
Dex Acquisitions Depot
on
Meor Ain
. Together they broke into the depot's ultra-security brig and took the
Clawdite
Tunga Arpagion with them
aboard the
Shadow Hawk
. Corbin and the others were able to convince him to pose as
Moff
Tan Hubi
while they used him to free
King
Lee-Char
. With Kast, Corbin and Gor were able to infiltrate the
Moncaladrome
through the refresher. Posing as an attendant, Corbin bluffed his way past a officer by allowing her to dry her hands on Gor. Corbin was then given a tip, much to Gor's chagrin. They were able to take Moff Hubi and switch him with Tunga.
Corbin flew a
submarine
to
Strokill Prime
to rescue Lee-Char. Gor was able to use Hubi's bio-signature to get past Strokill Prime's defenses, in addition to codes provided by Queen Trios.
Corbin flew the submarine past a
monstrous fish
. Corbin watched as Kast allowed the fish to swallow him. After Gor rescued Kast from the fish, Corbin took the submarine to the
Dominion base
and docked inside it. Corbin and Mira stunned several of the guards. Unfortunately, they discovered that the king was on life-support and could not be moved.
Corbin and Kast argued about their next move. Corbin and Kast searched for recording automaton so Mira could deliver Lee-Char's words to his people. Corbin found one and recorded Lee-Char's speech. After the guards killed Lee-Char, Corbin and the others returned to the submarine to escape. On Mon Cala, Corbin and the others met up with 3PO and
Tunga Arpagion
. They were confronted by Grand Admiral Urtya and several of his people who demanded Lee-Char's recording. After Urtya stunned Mira, Corbin allowed him to take Lee-Char's recording. As Corbin flew the
Shadow Hawk
away from Mon Cala, they were joined by the Mercantile fleet and a pair of Dominion Cruisers.
As the Dominion sent in bombers to destroy the
Shadow Hawk
and the Mercantile fleet, Corbin was contacted by Urtya, who agreed to rebel against the Dominion. They were then joined by the Free Worlds Coalition fleet under Ackbar, that would protect the fleet. Corbin told Mira to get into one of the
Shadow Hawk
'
s turrets and destroy the bombers. Corbin saw that the Dominion was targeting the Mon Calamari's escape pods and took the
Shadow Hawk
to stop several TIEs before they could destroy the pods. After the
Aurora Flare
blew up and destroyed one of the Dominion Cruisers, Corbin had the
Shadow Hawk
jump into voidspace and escape with both the Free Worlds Coalition fleet and the mercantile fleet. Corbin dropped Tunga off at the
Cosmatanic Steppes
.
Escape from the Mako-Ta Space Docks
At the same time as a Coalition party to celebrate the expanded Coalition fleet at the Mako-Ta Space Docks, Corbin, Gor, and 3PO went to the
Ring of Kafrene
to meet with an
Alaphani
in a bar for a deal for a set of
transponder codes
. LQ-9M translated the
Alaphani language
for Corbin, and the deal went well. During the closing phases of the deal, Corbin briefly joked about giving LQ-9M to the Alaphani for 10 percent off. LQ-9M was concerned by this, but Corbin explained he was joking and that no Alaphani would accept that deal. Gor then approached 3PO and Corbin to alert them that two of Jabba the Hutt's bounty hunters were in the bar. After hearing this, Corbin finished the deal, and the trio quickly left. The two bounty hunters followed, but Corbin and Gorath hid behind their field of view to surprise them. Corbin had LQ-9M take the bounty hunters' weapons, and as the trio ran back to the
Shadow Hawk
, Corbin yelled back and promised that Jabba would get his money.
LQ-9M put the bounty hunters' weapons on the table on board, and Corbin assured Gorath that they were not missing the party. He then explained to his partner that he had a contact who would pay them week for the set of codes. As the
Shadow Hawk
Gor reminded Corbin that Joren Kast had a role at the party, as he and other Starhawk pilots planned to do a flyby for the gathered rebel allies. Corbin admitted he would have liked to see it as well, and the
Shadow Hawk
left for the Space Docks.
Corbin arrived at the docks and found the Coalition fleet under attack by the Dominion's
Death Squadron
. Mira said that Queen Trios had betrayed them and disabled all the cruisers and their hangar bays. Mira told Corbin to fly into a bay door in order to open it. Corbin, despite his better judgment, took the
Shadow Hawk
close to Dominion Cruiser and several TIEs following him as he made a run towards one of the cruisers. The
Shadow Hawk
then came under attack by Lord Velgor in his
TIE Advanced
.
Corbin was able to evade Velgor while Gor tried to shoot him down. Corbin flew the
Shadow Hawk
towards a
cruiser
as Velgor fired several missiles at him. However, Velgor was able to force the
Shadow Hawk
away from the cruiser's hangar doors. After Commander
Lajaie
's cruiser was destroyed, Corbin decided to send a messenger to Mira by sending 3PO by escape pod. Corbin flew the
Shadow Hawk
through the cruiser's debris, hoping Velgor would not follow him. Gor was able to launch 3PO to land on the
Concordium
.
Corbin received helped from Kast's newly formed
Rogue Squadron
. Corbin led the squadron in taking out the TIEs.
Corbin was forced to land the
Shadow Hawk
on Ackbar's cruiser after taking heavy damage from Velgor.
He then took a
Flight suit
and took an Starhawk and rejoined the fight. Corbin and Kast came across a TIE that was acting weird and realized that an ally was aboard. Corbin and Kast led the TIE back to the cruiser. There, they learned that Mira was the pilot and got her to the
Concordium
'
s bridge. The
Concordium
was able to escape into voidspace.
Corbin, Kast, and the rest of Rogue Squadron returned to help the surviving cruisers. Corbin defended Mira and Kast's ship as they boarded the base to rescue Mothma and the other ambassadors. During the retreat, Corbin and R2's Starhawk was damaged but was rescued by a rebel
Quasar Fire
-class carrier.
As the carrier went into voidspace, it engines were fried and cruiser had to land on
Corosi 7
. Corbin was unable to contact Ackbar or Gor due to the Coalition fleet being kept hidden throughout the Outer Rim. Despite that, Corbin was then given a promotion to the rank of
colonel
by Mothma, though he refused it. Corbin then came up with plan to find Gor and the
Shadow Hawk
. Corbin took Mira and Kast to Nar Shadaa to meet with Starros, who agreed to help them.
Starros was forced to drop them off on
Hubin
, where they were stranded for weeks.
They eventually stole a shuttle when SCAR Squadron arrived. The team then hunted the remaining rebels and arrived at the
Kaliida Nebula
and met
Home One
.
Later, Venn came up with a plan to destabilize Shu-Torun's industry. She recruited her team, the tech
Meorti
, actor Tunga Arpagion, and the Partisans to pose as Queen Trios and disrupt the
Spike
.
Investigating Lotho Minor
"
And I'm not giving up on this planet yet.
"
―Mira Venn
Dax, Mira, Joren, Gorath, and LQ-9M, were sent by the Alliance to Restore the Concordium to investigate the planet
Lotho Minor
for a new base. During the investigation, they ran into native automatons and a male
Duros
named
Akar Duel
. Their actions drove Dax and the rest from Lotho Minor knowing that the planet was not a good location for a new rebel base.
Coalition duty on Kryos
"
Listen sweetheart, I'm no hero. I saw a fight and pointed my ship at it.
"
―Corbin's response to
Corwi Selgrothe
asking what the Coalition meant to him
Corbin converses with Loquim while repairing the
Shadow Hawk
alongside Gor in Echo Base.
Over three years after the Void Core's destruction at Kast's hand at the Battle of Yavin, the Alliance established a new base in the
Anoat sector
on the ice planet
Kryos
, far from the Dominion's reach.
The base was aptly designated
Echo Base
, as it was carved into the rocky caverns of the
Clabburn Range
of mountains. Within months of the new base being established, Corbin, Venn, Kast, and the others of Venn's team were deployed by Coalition High Command to Kryos aboard the
Shadow Hawk
.
Corbin and Kast scouted out the land around the base on regular recon missions as a part of their duties, using reptilian
tauntauns
as mounts to better traverse the freezing landscape.
Corbin took well to mounting and riding the unruly tauntauns and was sent on a reconnaissance mission in order to locate a
ship
that had gone missing a month before during a snow storm. The deflector shield which protected Echo Base from attack had been damaged and made inoperative, and the equipment aboard the lost ship was needed to repair the shield's generator. At dawn, Corbin, Venn, and the rebel veteran Twi'lek General
Hera Syndulla
ventured into the frigid mountains in search of the lost ship. Throughout the day, however, Corbin and Syndulla mastered tauntaun riding, while Venn had trouble controlling her beast and—to Corbin's amusement—was de-
saddled
. While Corbin helped the Princess re-mount her tauntaun, he informed the search team that night was fast approaching and they would soon have to return to Echo Base empty-handed. For her part, Venn insisted they couldn't return without first finding the lost ship. As she spoke, Venn again lost control of her tauntaun and stumbled backwards and fell from a steep embankment into a pit. Panicked, Corbin and Syndulla rushed to the cliff edge and were relieved to see the princess unharmed, having literally stumbled across the lost Coalition ship at the bottom of the pit. Corbin and Syndulla made their way safely to the bottom of the pit, arriving at Venn's position minutes later, and learned that Venn had found the equipment that could be used to repair the shield generator, and had fought off a massive carnivorous
wampa
. The trio then returned to Echo Base victorious.
Corbin took well to riding tauntauns on Kryos.
At some point, Corbin and his allies ran into the bounty hunter
Skorr
on
Ord Mantell
. The encounter convinced him to leave and fulfill his debt to Jabba, so he began repairing the
Shadow Hawk
on Kryos. Believing himself to be leaving, he, after scouting around the frozen wastes of Kryos with Kast under the callsign
Echo Seven
, had a heated conversation with Mira, claiming that she had romantic feelings towards him. After this debate, Corbin learned Joren had gone missing on patrol. Riding out on
a tauntaun named
Murra
in the midst of snowstorm, Dax found Joren had been attacked by a wampa.
After Murra collapsed
and died from the cold,
Dax used Joren's flux blade to cut the animal open and placed him inside to prevent him from freezing to death. After bringing Joren back to recover from his injuries, Dax and Gorath went out to investigate a signal and encountered an
Dominion probe automaton
. Realizing the Dominion had discovered their location, the Coalition forces decided to evacuate. During the
ensuing battle
, Dax rescued Mira from the collapsed command center, leading her towards her transport. A tunnel collapse cut off their way there, so Dax, Gor, Mira, and LQ-9M escaped on the still-unrepaired
Shadow Hawk
.
On the run from the Dominion
"
What are you doing? You're not actually going into an asteroid field?
"
"
They'd be crazy to follow us, wouldn't they?
"
―Warden Mira Venn and Dax Corbin
Dax and Mira share a kiss aboard the
Shadow Hawk
.
In space, the
Shadow Hawk
was pursued by several Dominion Cruisers and Razor drones. While Corbin was working on repairs, the ship drifted into an
asteroid field
. Claiming that the Imperials would be "crazy" to follow them, Corbin flew into the asteroid field, which would cause extensive damage to the pursuing Dominion Fleet. After the immediately pursuing Razor drones were destroyed by asteroids, Corbin landed the
Shadow Hawk
in a cave on a large asteroid, where he continued to work on repairs. While repairing the
Shadow Hawk
, Dax and Mira shared a kiss before being interrupted by LQ-9M.
Inside the cave, Mira saw something outside the ship, so she, Dax, and Gorath went out to investigate. Discovering that
mynocks
were the cause, Gorath accidentally fired a shot into the cave, which caused a tremor. Corbin realized something wasn't right; he fired his pulse rifle into the ground, causing another tremor. The three returned to the
Shadow Hawk
, flying out of the cave, which they discovered was actually the inside of an
exogorth
slug
named
Sy-O
. Although Sy-O attempted to keep them inside the internal ecosystem of itself by closing its mouth, the
Shadow Hawk
escaped, with Corbin and the others never knowing Sy-O had loved them as friends despite their brief stay.
Fleeing back towards the Imperials, Corbin flew straight towards the bridge of the Dominion Cruiser
Avenger
, then hid on the back of the bridge. The ruse worked; the Imperials were unable to find the ship. Preparing to spread about the stellar realm to search for the
Shadow Hawk
, the Dominion fleet dumped its trash, and Corbin detached the
Shadow Hawk
, which floated off with the rest of the trash. Corbin then decided to go to
Cloud City
, a
tibanna
gas mine on
Bespin
run by his old friend Lando Calrissian. Unknown to the rebels, however, Kael Ryn had also hidden his ship, the
Slave I
, among the Dominion's trash, and followed them to Cloud City.
Imprisonment in carbonite
"
I love you."
"I know.
"
―Mira Venn and Dax Corbin
Dax Corbin greets Lando Calrissian on Cloud City.
On Cloud City, Dax met his old friend Lando Calrissian, who despite showing initial hostility, embraced his old friend. Calrissian provided the rebels with lodging and promised to repair the
Shadow Hawk
. While Dax said he didn't trust Lando, due to his knowledge of Calrissian being a scoundrel, he appeared to be at ease in Cloud City, while Mira felt suspicious due to LQ-9M going missing and then reappearing in pieces. Calrissian then invited Corbin, Venn, and Gorath for refreshments, and told them about a deal that would keep the Dominion out of Cloud City. To Corbin's surprise, he led them into a room with Xarn Velgor. Corbin immediately drew his pulse rifle and fired several shots at the Shade Lord, but Velgor blocked the shots and pulled the pulse rifle out of Corbin's hands with the Synth Flux.
Corbin was then tortured by the Dominion, but he claimed they didn't even ask him any questions. When placed in a cell, Calrissian arrived and told the prisoners that Corbin would be handed over to Fett, who would take him to Jabba, and that they were a trap set by Velgor for Dax and Mira's friend Joren Kast. Angrily, Corbin attacked his "friend", but was restrained.
Velgor planned to take Kast to his master,
Galactic Overlord
Sheev Draven Nul
, by encasing him in
carbonite
. Unsure if a human could survive the process, he decided to test it on Corbin. Tearfully, Mira confessed her love to Dax, to which he simply replied that he knew. Mira, Gorath, Calrissian, Fett, and Velgor looked on as Dax was lowered into the carbon-freezing chamber and frozen. He survived the freezing process and fell into hibernation. He was then handed over to Fett and loaded onto
Slave I
. Despite a rescue attempt by Mira, Lando, and Gor, Fett escaped Cloud City, taking the frozen form of Dax Corbin with him.
Stolen by Qi'ra
"
They all love him. Hate him. Need him. Owe him or are owed
by
him. Dax Corbin unites the stellar realm. And that, right now, is
exactly
what I need. Dax always wanted to save me. Now he'll get his chance.
"
―Lady Qi'ra, to Margo
Due to the instability of the carbonite freezing process, Dax began to melt aboard
Slave I
, forcing Fett to make an emergency landing on Nar Shaddaa. He met with
Doc Ragon
, a doctor with whom Fett had a history, and the
Besalisk
tasked Fett with killing
Wyrmen Lictor
in exchange for his help. As he fought against various
pit
warriors, Doc managed to halt the melting and keep Dax alive. However, he was then killed by a group of assailants who took Dax and left Nar Shaddaa.
After securing Dax, they arrived at the
Vermillion
, where he was delivered to the reformed Crimson Dawn and its new leader, Qi'ra, who intended to use Corbin to lure together major galactic players for her
own plans
. With Dax in her custody, Qi'ra sent out a message to various factions in the criminal underworld, inviting them to an
auction
on
Jekara
. After delivering the message, Qi'ra then explained to her associate
Margo
that Dax was directly connected to almost every powerful faction in the stellar realm. Thus, he would be useful to both her and Crimson Dawn.
Still, Qi'ra fully intended for Corbin to end up back with the Coalition and ensured his location was leaked to agent
Amilyn Holdo
. She also placed a tracker on his carbonite slab.
Once the representatives of the various syndicates arrived, Qi'ra thanked them for coming as she introduced herself and officially announced Crimson Dawn's return. She then revealed Dax to the crowd and started the bidding at one hundred thousand credits. After multiple bids from different parties, the present members of the Hutt Clan began to bid over one another with increasingly higher offers. But this soon ended when Jabba bid with one million credits. With no other bids, Qi'ra congratulated Jabba and told him that the smuggler was his. However, the auction was then interrupted by the arrival of Xarn Velgor. Guarded by death troopers, Velgor declared that Dax belonged to him, to which Qi'ra smiled and decided to see where this would go.
After Jabba conceded his ownership of Dax to the Shade Lord, Qi'ra briefly
sparred
with Velgor before being knocked into Dax and incapacitated. But before Velgor could kill Qi'ra, he suddenly sensed Joren's presence through the Synth Flux.
After establishing communication with Joren's Starhawk, Velgor informed him that he had Dax in his possession. He then warned his son that if he did not come to him, then he would cut his friend in half.
But Joren refused and told Velgor to come to him instead. Velgor conceded, ordering General
Romodi
to bring Dax aboard his star destroyer and prepare his fighter.
Dax was then loaded onto an Dominion shuttle before it took off from Jekara.
After being brought aboard the
Executor
, Dax was transported to the ship's detention level under heavy guard. However, a surprise attack from the Hutt fleet allowed Mira, Lando and Gorath to infiltrate Velgor's flagship and eliminate the legion vanguard guarding Dax. But before they could escape with him, they were once again intercepted by Fett. One of the Hutt ships then blasted a hole in the room they were in, causing Dax to be sucked out into space. Utilizing his jetpack, Fett pursued Dax all the way down to the surface of Jekara. He then fished Dax up
before he hit the sea
and returned him to
Slave I
before flying him to Vorath Prime, fighting off IG-88
and finally turning him in to Jabba. Though Fett suggested dumping Dax into the
Great Pit of Carkoon
, Jabba ultimately decided to put the smuggler on display in his palace.
Rescue and marriage
Rescue from Jabba
Prelude
"
Over and over, he fought and risked his life and sacrificed and personally saved the entire
Alliance
more than once. He stayed because even if he couldn't admit it, even to himself, he
believes
in this cause. Dax Corbin's a smuggler because he likes it, but he's a rebel because he's Dax Corbin. And Dax Corbin complains and jokes and is generally half-useless…but he doesn't leave. That's inspiring. He
matters
. To the Coalition…and to me.
"
―Mira Venn, on Dax Corbin
Through her tracker, Qi'ra learned that Corbin had been brought to Jabba's Palace and discovered he had been placed on the Hutt's wall as a decoration. Traveling to the
Shadow Hawk
, where everyone aboard believed Corbin had died in the fall to Jekara, Qi'ra revealed Corbin's survival and imprisonment in Jabba's Palace. Venn agreed to wait for Qi'ra to confirm the time was right to launch a rescue operation, knowing she was needed with the Alliance and could not spend time on a rescue mission at the moment. Qi'ra promised to reach out, even arguing that Corbin would be safe in Jabba's Palace while the rebels waged their war with the Dominion,
but Crimson Dawn would be destroyed in the
Battle at Amaxine Station
.
Later, the Free Worlds Coalition began preparations for the mission. Mira, Gorath, and DR-7X sought the help of Maz Kanata, but were forced into a brief skirmish with the
Ubese
bounty hunter
Boushh
. After quickly overpowering him, Mira took his clothes, helmet, and thermal detonator as a disguise to sneak into Jabba's palace.
The rescue
"
Victims of the almighty sarlacc: His Excellency hopes that you will die honorably. But should any of you wish to beg for mercy, the great Jabba the Hutt will now listen to your pleas.
"
"
Loquim, you tell that slimy piece of worm-ridden filth he'll get no such pleasure from us. Right?
"
―LQ-9M, translating for Jabba the Hutt, and Dax Corbin
Dax freed from the carbonite by Mira
After almost a full year in captivity,
Corbin was released from the carbonite by Mira, disguised as the bounty hunter Boushh. Due to hibernation sickness, Corbin was temporarily blind, but recognized Jabba's laugh when he revealed himself to be watching the rescue. Corbin was then placed in a prison cell with his partner Gorath, while Mira was taken as a personal slave by Jabba. After Mira was captured, Joren Kast arrived, and faced off against Jabba's prized Rancor, killing it. In rage, Jabba sentenced Kast, Corbin, and the Thoran to be fed to the
sarlacc
in the Great Pit of Carkoon.
At the pit, Jabba offered the prisoners one last chance to plead for mercy, to which Corbin replied that Jabba would receive no such pleasure from them. Kast then offered Jabba one last chance to free them, which he denied. DR-7X then released
Joren's flux blade
; Joren caught it and began a battle. In the ensuing struggle, Dax was still mostly blind, rendering him unable to contribute much. However, after being told by Gorath that Kael Ryn had joined the battle, Dax unintentionally swung an axe into Fett's jetpack, sending him flying into Jabba's sail barge and then rolling into the sarlacc. After a pulse rifle shot knocked Lando, disguised as a palace guard, off the skiff they were on, Dax lowered the axe for Lando to grab, but Calrissian's foot was caught by one of the sarlacc's tentacles. Claiming his vision had "gotten a lot better," Dax shot the tentacle and pulled Lando back up into the skiff. Joren then rescued Mira, Drex, and Loquim from the sail barge and destroyed it, and then the rebels escaped.
The Battle of Veldara
"
I love you."
"I know.
"
―Dax Corbin and Mira Venn during the Battle of Veldara
Corbin on Veldara
After being rescued, Dax joined the Alliance and was given the rank of General. As part of the attack on the
second Void Core
, with Kast, Venn, and Gorath, Dax commandeered the
Tydirium
, a captured Dominion shuttle, and took a Coalition strike team to the forest moon of Veldara to disable the shields protecting the superweapon. Shortly after landing on the forest moon, the strike team encountered a group of
scout troopers
. Corbin attempted to sneak up on them, but failed to do so, alerting them and allowing two to escape. Joren and Mira hopped on a speeder to pursue them, leaving Corbin and the rest of the strike team behind.
Joren eventually made his way back to the rest of the Coalition forces, without information on Mira. Joren, Dax, Gor, Drex, and Loquim set off to find Mira, while Dax sent the rest of the strike team to move towards the shield generator. While looking, the rebels came across
a
verkle
trap
laid by the native
Moorlings
. Despite Corbin urging him to ignore the dead animal,
the verkle's smell ended up being irresistible to Gorath,
who triggered the trap. Upon releasing the rebels, the furry tribesmen believed LQ-9M to be a god, and they attempted to cook the others at a banquet in his honor. However, Joren used the Synth Flux to levitate the "god" in a demonstration of his "magic", frightening the Moorlings into releasing the others. At the village, the Coalition forces found Mira, and LQ-9M managed to befriend the Moorlings. Joren then left the other rebels to confront his father, Xarn Velgor.
Dax Corbin with Mira Venn after the Battle of Veldara
The next day, Corbin's group met up with the main Coalition strike team at the shield generator, but Moorling scouts informed them of a less-guarded rear entrance. After three of the four guards were distracted by the Moorling
Paploo
, the Coalition forces neutralized the last guard and entered the shield bunker. But the operation proved to be a trap, and Corbin and the Coalition forces were captured. However, the Dominion had not expected the Moorlings to attack, which allowed the Coalition forces to escape. For most of the battle, Dax stayed at the door, trying to open it. After DR-7X was damaged while attempting to open it, Dax unsuccessfully tried to hotwire the door open. He then impersonated the pilot of an AT-ST that had been captured by Gorath and two Moorlings, getting the Imperials to open the doors. Dax was then able to place detonators inside the shield generator, destroying it and leaving the Void Core vulnerable to attack. Dax and Mira witnessed the Void Core's destruction, and she revealed to him that Joren was her brother. Corbin participated with the other Coalition forces and Moorlings in the victory celebration held that night.
Aftermath of the battle
Following the battle, Corbin, Gorath, and DR-7X were sent by Venn to
General Syndulla's camp
to obtain food rations for the Moorlings as a means to keep the furry creatures from making a meal out of the captured legion vanguard. While at Syndulla's camp, Syndulla forced Corbin to admit that her ship the
Ghost
was superior to the
Shadow Hawk
in order to get the rations.
The morning after the Battle of Veldara, Corbin led the Pathfinders on an
assault
on an
Dominion outpost
on the far side of Veldara. Knowing that the Dominion outpost was preparing for a counterattack, the rebel commanders dispatched Dax Corbin on a mission to destroy the base. Dax outlined a plan for the Pathfinders and their Moorling allies to strike the outpost with limited air cover. During the assault, the rebel pilot Lieutenant
Shara Bey
served as the transport's pilot. The Pathfinders quickly wiped out the garrison, and Corbin uncovered a lot of data in the outpost's computers, which led him to conclude that the war was not over.
Two days after the Battle of Veldara, Corbin proposed to Venn, who accepted the
marriage proposal
. While in the Bright Tree Village,
Dax and Mira were then married; their ceremony was attended by only people they trusted.
Corbin and Venn departed Veldara to have their honeymoon on the luxury
Star Cruiser
known as the
Halcyon
, where they
encountered
an Dominion force. She also told him she had met Qi'ra, much to his surprise.
As a wedding gift to his wife, Corbin reconfigured some of the crew quarters on the
Shadow Hawk
to include a galley.
New Concordium
Honoring a life debt
Gathering intelligence
Before the formal establishment of the
New Concordium
, Corbin, LQ-9M, and the Pathfinders participated in a
raid
on an ISB black site at the
Wretch
of
Tayron
. During the assault, the Pathfinders managed to down an AT-ST walker, which caused a breach in the facility's hall. This allowed Corbin and his men to enter the base and secure the computers before the
base commander
could initiate a purge of the computers. With the aid of LQ-9M, the Pathfinders were able to hack into the Dominion data files and discovered that the Dominion had begun to execute
Operation: Cinder
, a posthumous order issued by the Overlord to devastate several worlds including
Aqualis
as retribution for his demise.
The Pathfinders' actions enabled the Free Worlds Coalition to deploy forces to halt Operation: Cinder, saving many lives and worlds.
Dax Corbin talks to Maz Kanata on Takodana.
Several months after the Battle of Veldara, Dax arrived at Maz Kanata's castle on Takodana to rendezvous with an Dominion defector named
Ralsius Paldora
, who had stolen intelligence crucial to the liberation of Kashyyyk. The Empire had quickly discovered his treachery and followed him. Recovering the data cubes and fighting their way back to the
Shadow Hawk
, Dax and Ralsius were
saved
by the arrival of
Inferno Squad
, buying them time to escape the planet.
False leads
"
C'mon, Gor. Set new coordinates. It's time to get you home.
"
―Dax Corbin to Gorath, on the way to liberate Kashyyyk
Some time later, Dax and Gorath received additional intelligence from the smuggler
Imra
that the Thoran homeworld of
Kashyyyk
would have a decreased Dominion presence.
Against the advice of his wife, Mira, the two friends gathered several fellow smugglers and people who owed them favors for a
hastily planned campaign to liberate
the planet. However, this turned out to be disinformation from the Dominion to lure Dax and his companions into a trap.
While Gor attempted to board a gunship captained by the Thoran
pirate
Kirratha
, they were attacked by two Dominion Cruisers and several Razor drones. The Imperials disabled Kirratha's engines, leaving Gor and Kirratha stranded in space. Dax managed to escape, but Gorath was captured and locked in an Dominion prison called
Ashmead's Lock
. While traveling through the edge of
Wild Space
, Dax contacted his wife to discuss the recent developments. However, he came under attack from Dominion forces and lost communication with Mira.
Warden Mira was unable to get help from the New Concordium since they were preoccupied with fighting against the Solar Dominion. Since Dax had resigned his commission as general in the
New Concordium Defense Flux
, he was low on their list of priorities. Desperate, Mira turned to the New Concordium starfighter pilot
Norra Wexley
for help. Norra and Captain Wedge Antilles traveled to Corbin's last known coordinates in Wild Space but found themselves under attack by Dominion forces. After being rescued by the New Concordium Starfleet, Norra resigned her commission in the Starfleet to find Corbin out of respect for Warden Mira.
Finding new allies
Norra, her son
Temmin Wexley
, his battle automaton
Mister Bones
, the
Zabrak
bounty hunter
Jas Emari
, the former Dominion
loyalty officer
Sinjir Rath Velus
, and the New Concordium soldier
Jom Barell
traveled to the planet
Irudiru
after learning that Corbin had last been sighted at
Warrin Station
. There, they found Corbin while scouting the compound of the
Siniteen
prison designer
Golas Aram
. At a nearby
cantina
, Corbin informed his allies that he had discovered that Gorath was being held at Ashmead's Lock and that Aram was responsible for designing the prison. During their initial encounter, Corbin was accosted by Mister Bones, who mistook him for a threat.
Working together, Corbin and Norra's team managed to breach the compound and capture Aram. Under duress, Aram told the rebels how to enter Ashmead's Lock. However, he omitted the password that would allow them unhindered passage inside the prison. Traveling to the Kashyyyk system in the
Shadow Hawk
and Emari's ship
Halo
, Corbin and Norra's team gained clearance to land at Ashmead's Lock by claiming to be a repair team. Upon entering the prison, Dax and the rebels were accosted by the prison's computer security system
SOL-GDA
, which dispatched
automatons
to integrate them.
Temmin discovered that the prison was being powered by the prisoners who were being housed inside stasis cells. Using this information, Dax and Norra managed to disable the power generator. As a result, Dax and Norra's team were able to free Gorath and a hundred other prisoners including Norra's husband and Temmin's father
Brentin Lore Wexley
. While Norra, Temmin, and Mister Bones took the liberated prisoners back to Chandrila on the
Shadow Hawk
, Dax stayed behind with Gorath, Jas, Sinjir, and Jom to complete their mission to liberate the Thorans. Unknown to Dax and his allies, the Dominion
Fleet Admiral
Gallius Rax
had inserted inorganic bio-chip implants inside the prisoners as part of a
plot
to assassinate the New Concordium leadership.
Liberating Kashyyyk
Dax and his team learned that
Grand Moff
Lozen Tolruck
had fitted all of the Thorans on Kashyyyk with
inhibitor chips
. Tolruck controlled these inhibitor chips with a control module which he kept on his person. For the next month, Dax and his team spent their time searching for Tolruck's island fortress and gathering Thoran exiles and smugglers for a mass uprising. On one mission, Jom Barell was captured by Tolruck's forces and imprisoned at his
island fortress
. While Sinjir entered the island fortress to free Jom and disabled Tolruck's control module, Dax and his team disabled the deflector shield generator at
Camp Sardo
. Despite being attacked by Dominion forces, Jas managed to take out the shield generator.
After Sinjir disabled Tolruck's control module, a mass planetwide Thoran uprising broke out. The Thorans and their rebel allies quickly overwhelmed the outnumbered Dominion garrison. When the orbiting Dominion Dominion Cruisers began bombarding Kashyyyk, Dax, Gorath, and their fellow rebels managed to evacuate the civilians underground. They then took a ship loaded with
webweaver
spiders
aboard the Dominion Cruiser
Dominion
and fought their way through the ship's corridors. However, they were captured by legion vanguard who brought them before
Vice Admiral
Domm Korgale
.
Before the admiral could eliminate Dax and his team, New Concordium reinforcements arrived in the Kashyyyk system in the form of Mira and the
Shadow Hawk
, Antilles' Phantom Squadron, and Admiral Ackbar's flagship,
Home One
. Dax and Gorath managed to break free of their captors and seize control of the
Dominion
. Dax and his team then turned the ship's guns on the Dominion Cruiser
Vitiator
. As a result, the remaining Dominion Cruiser
Neutralizer
surrendered to Admiral Ackbar.
Following the battle, Dax and Mira experienced an emotional reunion aboard the
Dominion
. Returning to Kashyyyk, Dax, Gorath, and Mira learned about the Liberation Day attacks on Chandrila. With the Thorans undertaking mop-up operations against the remaining Dominion holdouts on Kashyyyk, Dax insisted that Gorath stay behind and reunite with his
family
. However, he told Gorath that he would return if he ever needed help. Dax then left Kashyyyk aboard the
Shadow Hawk
with Mira and their unborn child,
Ben Corbin
.
Balancing family and duty
Over the next several months leading to the
Battle of Jakku
, Dax Corbin and Warden Mira prepared for the coming of their child. With Mira approaching labor, Corbin hired a protocol automaton named
T-2LC
(or "Elsie") as a nanny. One day, Corbin bought his wife a sack of
jogan fruits
. Mira was annoyed with Corbin for overdoing it and reminded him that Dr.
Harter Kalonia
had said to incorporate jogan into her diet and not to replace everything with it. While chatting with his wife about the liberation of Kashyyyk and galactic politics, Dax and Mira were contacted on
hologram
by the New Concordium operative Norra Wexley. Norra said that she was traveling to the
Inner Rim
world of
Jakku
to hunt down Grand Admiral
Rae Sloane
, the purported instigator behind the Liberation Day attacks on Chandrila.
Norra Wexley's team
subsequently discovered that most of the Solar Dominion had retreated to Jakku. While Norra, the bounty hunter Jas Emari, and the battle automaton Mister Bones infiltrated Jakku, Temmin and Sinjir returned to Chandrila with news about the Dominion presence. They visited Mira's domicile and warned Warden Mira and Dax Corbin about the Dominion. Temmin tried to convince Corbin to lend him his famous freighter, the
Shadow Hawk
. However, Corbin thought that the mission was too risky and was unwilling to risk his ship.
Before the argument could escalate, Chancellor Mon Mothma entered the domicile and asked Temmin and Sinjir about the Dominion presence on Jakku. Following the meeting, Temmin and Sinjir managed to convince Dax and Mira to lend them the
Shadow Hawk
. However, the two were stopped from leaving Chandrila by
Senate Guards
who had been dispatched by Senator
Tolwar Wartol
, a rival of Chancellor Mothma. Wartol had secretly installed a listening device inside T-2LC. In an attempt to discredit Chancellor Mothma, Wartol leaked news about the Dominion presence to
HoloNet News
. Corbin and Mira learned about the leak while watching the morning news.
Unmasking a conspiracy
Dax and Mira were later confronted by an angry Sinjir, who believed that they had double-crossed him and Temmin. Dax and Mira managed to set the record straight. They then realized that Senator Wartol's leak and the Senate guards stopping Sinjir and Temmin were linked. When Sinjir voiced his suspicion that there was a spy bug in Mira's domicile, Dax did not believe it, but Mira shared Sinjir's suspicion. Sinjir brought his former lover
Conder Kyl
, a slicer, to Mira's domicile. Conder found that the listening device was inside T-2LC. Following the discovery, Dax told Elsie to hold still and warned that it was going to sting a little.
After removing the listening device and handing it to Chancellor Mothma, Dax led a team consisting of Sinjir, Temmin, Conder, and Jom Barell to the New Concordium's new capital of
Nakadia
. Their
mission
was to investigate the five senators who had opposed Chancellor Mothma's resolution to send military forces to Jakku:
Ashmin Ek
,
Rethalow
,
Dor Wieedo
,
Grelka Sorka
, and
Nim Tar
. While Sinjir and the others trailed four of the senators at a restaurant called
Izzik's
in Nakadia's capital
Quarrow
, Dax went to spy on Senator Dor Wieedo. The Rodian senator was aboard his ship at the northernmost spaceport outside Quarrow.
After several hours had passed, Corbin witnessed two
Nikto
and a
Klatooinian
approach Wieedo's ship. Unknown to Corbin, these aliens were members of the
Red Key Raiders
, a crime syndicate that was plotting with Black Sun to prolong the war. Corbin managed to warn his comrades. However, the criminals discovered his presence and locked him in a rubbish bin. Meanwhile, Conder was kidnapped by other Red Key gangsters. Sinjir and Temmin found Corbin and returned to the
Shadow Hawk
. Despite their setbacks, Jom managed to kidnap Senator Rethalow, who confessed to his dealings with Red Key and Black Sun.
Before they could spirit Rethalow away into hiding, Dax and his team members decided to rescue Conder,
Nim Tar's child
, and Sorka's
pet
jerba
. Conder used his
transceiver tooth
to transmit his coordinates to Sinjir. Dax and his team flew the
Shadow Hawk
through a red warehouse roof, which turned out to be the Red Key's base. After subduing the gangsters, they rescued Conder and Nim Tar's child. Posing as an adviser to the Chancellor, Sinjir transmitted a missive offering the five senators pardons and other political favors if they changed their votes during the re-vote scheduled to be held at the
Quarrow Senate house
. As a result, Mothma's resolution passed, and the New Concordium was able to dispatch military forces to Jakku.
War's end
"
Yeah. You, me, Joren. Gor. The
Shadow Hawk
. Those two walking talking garbage cans. It feels weird we're not part of it.
"
"
We've got our own adventure.
"
―Dax and Mira
Following the events on Nakadia, Dax returned home and reunited with his wife. When Mira praised him for "making things right", Corbin told her that the Battle of Jakku was happening. He predicted that it would be a big battle. In private, Dax confided that it felt strange that he, Mira, Joren, Gorath, LQ-9M, and DR-7X were not part of the final battle. Mira reassured him that they had their own adventure. Dax responded that it was the end of an era while Mira replied that it was the start of a new one.
The Battle of Jakku turned out to be a decisive victory for the New Concordium. Facing imminent defeat,
Grand Vizier
Mas Amedda
on Nexara City-world sent peace overtures to Chancellor Mothma. Dax and his wife attended the peace talks where Chancellor Mothma and Grand Vizier Amedda signed the
Dominion Instruments of Surrender
and the
Galactic Concordance
. The signing occurred during the third hour of Mira's labor, but she only told her husband after the ceremony was complete. Corbin rushed her to the birthing chamber in the heart of
Hanna City
.
Corbin with his wife and son
Mira gave birth to a son named Ben Corbin. Following a gathering of family and friends, Dax stared into his son's eyes and pondered how he had become a father. While Mira was showering in the next room, Corbin told his son that while the whole stellar realm was against them, they would still make it through okay. Though Corbin confided that he might not make a good father, he promised to keep them pointed in the right direction. Corbin also gave Ben his first lesson: sometimes doing the right thing doesn't mean following a straight line. Corbin told his baby son not to tell his mother.
When Ben cried, Dax tried to comfort him with a small
tooka doll
, but this failed to pacify the baby. Mira then heard her son crying. When Dax said that it was not his fault, Mira reassured him that babies cried. Dax sensed that Mira had a connection through the Synth Flux that he could never have with Ben. Dax suggested using the Synth Flux, but Mira advised him to pick up their son and snuggle him. Dax reluctantly snuggled his son and Ben stopped crying. When Dax sighed that he could never have the same Flux connection, Mira told him that fathers had a special connection with their offspring.
Fighting the Zantarrk Gang
"
You Heard the bounty hunter. Nobody's dying today.
"
―Dax Corbin
Corbin and Valance fought against the Zantarrk Gang.
Around
6 ABY
,
Corbin was a in
Alshuey Town
on
Guat'a
. There, he helped Valance after was thrown out of a bar by the
Zantarrk Gang
. Corbin sent out a warning shot against the gang members and saved Valance. The two of them then fought the Zantarrk in a gun fight. After dealing with the Zantarrk, Valance went with Corbin to the
Shadow Hawk
.
Baylan Skoll's gauntlet
Around
9 ABY
,
during the search for
Grand Admiral
Thrawn
, the former Keeper Knight and
General
turned mercenary
Baylan Skoll
listed Corbin's name in a device on his gauntlet, as well as the names of other key members of the
Heroes of Yavin
.
Starting a family
"
I'll be back. We're not done, you and I. We'll see each other again. I'm gonna be a father and no way my kid won't have you in his life.
"
―Dax Corbin parting ways with Gorath
Finding himself husband of an important politician, Dax passed the time by becoming a successful racing pilot.
On one occasion he returned to Corellia to compete in
The Gauntlet
, where he raced against
Greer Sonnel
and offered her a spot on his team for the
Five Sabers
race.
In the years after the end of the Galactic Civil War, Corbin became the owner of a
shipping company
and developed a reputation for being a vigilant and responsible owner. Outside of his work, Dax often raced for charity and sponsored several piloting matches. Due to his work, Corbin found himself traveling through different systems, working on ships.
Their son, Ben, proved to be Flux-sensitive and was sent to be trained by his uncle Joren Kast in the ways of the Synth Flux.
Meanwhile, his wife Mira served as a prominent
Senator
in the
Galactic Senate
on
Hosnian Prime
, the capital of the New Concordium. Dax and his family lived separate lives but he kept in touch with his wife via hologram.
Working toward someday
"
You really think you'd enjoy the life of a racer? Bouncing around from one system to the next, working on ships, never knowing where you're headed next?
"
"
Doesn't sound that different from being a member of the Free Worlds Coalition.
"
―Dax and Mira talking about their plans to travel the stellar realm together
For a time, Corbin managed the Five Sabers racing championship.
Twenty-four years after the Battle of Veldara, Dax Corbin traveled to the planet
Theron
to manage a prestigious racing championship called the Five Sabers. While staying in temporary quarters, he corresponded with Mira, who told her husband about the fractured state of the Galactic Senate. When Mira told Dax about her desire to resign from the Senate, Dax expressed puzzlement, since his wife had devoted much of her adult life to politics. Sensing Mira's frustration, Corbin invited his wife to take a break and travel around the stellar realm with him for three months. Despite their different personalities, Mira agreed that she needed a break from her senatorial career.
However, Mira was forced to suspend her vacation plans after her faction, the
Populists
, convinced her to stand for the position of
First Senator
. The rival
Centrist
faction, which supported a stronger central government, had successfully passed a motion calling for the creation of First Senator in order to end the deadlock plaguing the Concordium. Despite their initial opposition, the Populists, who preferred a more decentralized government, had decided to change tactics in order to prevent a Centrist from holding that office. Due to her prestige as a prominent former rebel leader, Mira had reluctantly agreed to allow an exploratory committee to explore her suitability for First Senator.
While Dax was repairing the rear engines of a starship, he was contacted by his wife. Corbin was mildly disappointed with his wife's change of plans but agreed that a Populist should serve as First Senator. Corbin also informed his wife that he was supervising the lunar relays of the Five Sabers. Due to the complicated nature of the repairs, Dax remarked that he wished that Greer Sonnel, a former racer and
Junior Sabers
champion who was serving as Mira's pilot, was still on his team. When Mira apologized for delaying their planned holiday, Dax reassured her that she was doing the right thing by putting duty to the New Concordium first. Mira promised to take a break with her husband "someday."
In addition to the First Senatorship, Mira was involved in a sensitive Senate investigation into the Nikto crime lord
Rinnrivin Di
's
cartel
, a new criminal outfit that was choking the economy of the
Gaulus sector
. Mira was assisted in this investigation by an unlikely ally, the idealistic Centrist senator
Ransolm Casterfo
. Over the course of their investigation, the two discovered that Rinnrivin was linked to a paramilitary faction called the
Amaxine warriors
. In an attempt to sow discord,
Arliz Hadrassian
, a former Razor drone pilot who had become the Amaxines' leader,
bombed
the
New Concordium senatorial complex
's
conference building
. Mira survived the bombing and transmitted a voice-message telling her husband that she was safe and that he did not need to come to Hosnian Prime. While on Theron, Corbin received news about the Senate bombing and saw footage of the damage. Later, Corbin contacted Mira to send his wife a goodwill message and to keep in touch.
While Mira was visiting
Ryloth
as part of her investigation, Dax Corbin sent a holomessage expressing hope that his wife's side project was going well. He informed her that the Five Sabers was heading to the fourth stage of the Sabers. Due to the sublight relay round, Dax told her that he would be out of comm range for a while. He also told her to take care of herself and complimented her.
Rescue on Sibensko
"
Sorry, I'm late, sweetheart.
"
"
Honey, you're right on time.
"
―Dax reuniting with his wife on Sibensko
Dax and Mira would cross paths again due to unforeseen circumstances. Later, the Centrist Senator Lady
Carise Sindian
, a secret agent of an Dominion remnant known as the
First Order
, discovered information about Mira's true parentage as the daughter of Xarn Velgor on
Birren
. Alarmed at the friendship between Ransolm Casterfo and Mira, Lady Carise shared this information with him, knowing Ransolm's hatred for Velgor. Feeling betrayed, Casterfo upstaged the Senate hearing into Mira's nomination for First Senator by revealing Mira's parentage to the assembled senators. This revelation destroyed Mira's credibility and forced her to withdraw her nomination.
Still determined to continue her investigation into Rinnrivin's cartel and the Amaxines, Mira and her team took leave to travel to
Sibensko
, where the two groups were hiding. There, Mira discovered that the Amaxines were funding Rinnrivin's cartel and were also marshaling troops, weapons, and starships for an attack on the New Concordium. During her
mission
, Mira was pursued by the Amaxine warriors and fled up a landing platform, where she was corned by two Amaxine starfighters. Dax arrived on a
racer
and rescued his wife. Together, the couple shot down the enemy fighters. One of the fighters, a
B-wing
, collided with the underwater base's ammunition depot, triggering a massive explosion that destroyed the underwater city and the Amaxine army.
Following the Sibensko mission, Dax and his wife returned to Mira's apartment in Hosnian Prime. When Mira expressed despair that many of her former colleagues and comrades were shunning her for simply being Velgor's daughter, Corbin reassured her that this storm would pass and that people would remember her for who she was. Dax counseled his wife to remember those friends who stuck by her and to ignore the rest of the stellar realm. When Dax criticized Casterfo, Mira sprung to her former friend's defense by pointing out that he was a decent man who hated Velgor. Dax then advised his wife to forget Casterfo and to use the political scandal as an opportunity to retire from politics for good.
However, Mira still had one piece of unfinished business left: the findings of her investigation into Rinnrivin Di's cartel and the Amaxines. Mira wanted to address the Senate, but she needed to gain a quorum from a majority of senators. While Dax thought that no more threats to the Concordium existed with the demise of Rinnrivin and the Amaxines, Mira suspected that there were other like-minded groups seeking to overthrow the Concordium. Dax counseled his wife to rest for the night, and the couple expressed their love for each other.
The following morning, the sports channels reported that Dax Corbin had left the
Theron system
after the end of the last Sabers round. There was also speculation on whether he would return for the final championship race. Later that evening, Dax bought his wife
Bilbringi
cheesy meat pies for dinner. While eating, the couple received news that Mira had received a quorum to address the Senate due to Casterfo's favorable vote. Dax then told his wife that things would improve and that he would stay with her for as long as she needed him. Mira reassured her husband that she would be able to handle the hearing on her own and told him that he could return to the Sabers championship. Shortly, Corbin returned to the Five Sabers race.
Cold War
Family strife
"
And Corbin… Did you know that's not even his real name? He's a
lie
.
"
―Ben Corbin, to Snoke, about his father
The loss of his son to the First Order and the deep flux left a lasting impact on Corbin.
At some point after the public revelations about Mira and Ben's relationship to Xarn Velgor,
Dax's son, Ben Corbin, was seduced by
Supreme Leader
Snoke
and eventually succumbed to the deep flux,
killing
the Keepers Joren was
training
. Taking the blame for Ben's fall, Joren
exiled himself
. Unable to cope with the loss of their son, Dax and Mira separated.
Dax and Gorath went back to being smugglers, which Dax claimed was "the only thing he was ever good at." Meanwhile, Mira became the leader of the
Resistance
, a paramilitary organization aimed at fighting the First Order, which had begun to pose a threat to the New Concordium. Dax then got himself into a lot of debt with various creditors including the notorious
Guavian Death Gang
and
Kanjiklub
.
Losing the
Shadow Hawk
"
Short-handed? I can help. I can be your co-pilot.
"
―Gannis Ducain to Dax Corbin
In
31 ABY
Corbin traveled to Maz's castle, where he spoke with Kanata about Gorath being unable to do a job with him.
Gannis Ducain
, who worked for Kanata, overheard and suggested he help on the job. The job was to deliver cargo to
Christophsis
. When Dax and Ducain arrived, Dax left Ducain to guard the
Shadow Hawk
whilst he met with his contact. Ducain, however, stole the
Shadow Hawk
. Ducain became a rich
gunrunner
. One day the
Irving Boys
stole the
Shadow Hawk
from him.
After losing the
Shadow Hawk
, Dax and Gorath obtained a
Baleen
-class heavy freighter
named the
Eravana
that they used for their smuggling operations and even assembled a crew to assist them. Dax spent the majority of his free time trying to track down the
Shadow Hawk
.
Smuggler's run
Confrontation with bounty hunters in the
Serendipity
Sometime later, Corbin and Gorath went to the
Serendipity
, a yacht that was owned by Leighton. There, Corbin engaged a trio of bounty hunters—
Strater
,
Hove
, and
Rigger
—sent to capture him on the
Serendipity
cantina, and told them about their mission to Cyrkon before they realized who he was. Once they were aware of Corbin's identity, Gorath snuck behind them and took hold of Rigger, only to send him flying to the
Serendipity
'
s door and knock Strater out with a punch, while Corbin disarmed Bobbie. Then, the Corellian told them to warn their possible employers (Ducain, the Irving Boys, the Guavians, etc.) that they were not afraid of them, before saying goodbye to Leighton and leaving the bar together.
Alderaanian data archive heist
Corbin, posing as a First Order officer aboard
Mek Nu'Tiv's cruiser
Corbin and Gorath stole a
Xi
-class light shuttle
from the
shipyard
of the
Fluzhi Clan
. They escaped from the shipyard and planned to use codes from
Finwej
to steal a weapons archive from
Mek Nu'Tiv
. Corbin hoped to use the archive to get several people they owed money to off their backs. Corbin, posing as a First Order officer, boarded Mek Nu'Tiv's cruiser and spoke with her and her crew. Nu'Tiv, however, realized that Corbin had used Finwej to get credits and had her crew hold him at gunpoint.
Retaking the
Shadow Hawk
"
The Keeper were real?
"
"
I used to wonder about that myself. Thought it was a bunch of mumbo jumbo. A magical power holding together good and evil, the deep flux and the light. Crazy thing is, it's true. The Synth Flux, the Keepers. All of it—it's all true.
"
―Rey and Dax Corbin
Corbin pleased to be back aboard the
Shadow Hawk
In
34 ABY
while transporting a cargo of three Rathtars aboard the
Eravana
for the King
Prana
, Dax and Gor found the
Shadow Hawk
floating in space near the desert planet of Jakku, pulling his prized vessel in with a tractor beam. Upon boarding their old vessel, the two went about inspecting their ship and found two stowaways, Jakku scavenger
Rey
and former First Order
legion vanguard
Finn
, who he assumed were the thieves who had stolen the
Shadow Hawk
. When Rey and Finn explained that they were going to take a automaton named
BB-8
to the Resistance as he had a map to the location of Corbin's old friend Joren Kast, Dax and Gor agreed to help them.
Corbin rediscovered the
Smuggler's Guide
that was previously owned by
Unkar Plutt
aboard the ship, thinking it was a bad omen that this thing keeps resurfacing. However, Gorath thought it was a good luck that it keeps coming back and was happy to see the logbook and kept opening different pages. Dax thought if that was true his life would have turned out differently. These reflections were recorded in the logbook.
Skirmish aboard the
Eravana
"
What are you going to do?
"
"
Same thing I always do, talk my way out of it.
"
―Rey and Dax Corbin
Dax Corbin trapped between two criminal factions aboard the
Eravana
Dax's freighter had been boarded by the Guavian Death Gang and Kanjiklub, whom Corbin was indebted to. Not wanting Rey and Finn to be involved, Dax placed both of them in the lower corridors of the freighter while he and Gor went to meet with the criminals, with BB-8 accompanying them. The smuggler tried but failed to convince both gangs that he would fix things eventually, with one of the gang leaders threatening to take away the
Shadow Hawk
as payback. Dax having just retrieved his vessel, responded that they would have to take it over his dead body. One of the criminals then recognized the BB-8 unit from a First Order bounty placed on it along with Rey and Finn, and demanded that Dax hand over the automaton and any stowaways he might have on board. Right before a firefight broke out, Rey accidentally released the rathtars, providing a diversion for an escape in the
Shadow Hawk
.
Journey to Takodana
"
She's run this watering hole for a thousand years. Maz is a bit of an acquired taste, so let me do the talking. And whatever you do, don't stare.
"
"
At what?
"
"Any
of it.
"
―Dax Corbin, Rey and Finn
Dax asks Maz Kanata to get BB-8 to the Resistance.
Dax explained that Joren tried to rebuild the Keepers of the Flux, but an apprentice of his turned to the deep flux and became Kylo Ren, destroying all that Joren had built, including the other
Padawans
that Joren had trained. Feeling responsible and blaming himself for the failure, Joren went into exile.
En route to Takodana, Dax reminisced about the
Shadow Hawk
, her corridors, fresh smell of thread grease, stale funk of the couch fabric. The way the aircycler makes the whine before it switches off and Gor's feet clomping all over the hollow deck plates. He thought about dropping the logbook off with Maz, but changed his mind. His thoughts were recorded in the
Smuggler's Guide
.
Later on the same route, the
Shadow Hawk
'
s hyperdrive cut out. Rey, Finn, and Gorath investigated and found a tracker near the
sensor pulse generator
. Finn removed the tracker, only to discover that it was rigged with explosives. Rey and Gor jettisoned the tracker bomb out the airlock, while Finn informed Dax of the situation and the
Shadow Hawk
jumped back into voidspace.
Scoundrel's redemption
"
And maybe she could carry on after I'm gone. Another roll of a dice. A scoundrel's redemption.
"
―Dax Corbin, in the
Smuggler's Guide
By the time they arrived on Takodana, Rey came to regard Corbin as a mentor and father figure.
Corbin handed Rey an
NN-14 pulse rifle pistol
, knowing she would be able to defend herself with it, and asked Rey what her name was. With the two formally introduced, Corbin said he was considering bringing on a new crew member. Impressed with Rey's piloting and repair abilities, he said he would consider offering her a job as a
second mate
aboard the
Shadow Hawk
. Rey was flattered by the offer and briefly excited by the prospect, but she turned it down because she had to return to Jakku to wait for her family, claiming that she'd already been away for too long already.
Takodana Castle
Corbin took Rey and Finn to meet his old friend Kanata at her castle. There, he learned that Kanata had found and kept the old flux blade that once belonged to Joren, and he asked where Kanata got the weapon, but Kanata dismissed him, saying it was a story for another time. While there, the First Order was alerted to BB-8's presence and attacked. During the ensuing battle, he killed several legion vanguard both with his DL-44 pulse rifle and
Gorath's
bowcaster
, Corbin, Finn, and Gorath were initially captured, but escaped when the Resistance, also alerted to BB-8's presence, counterattacked. Despite the First Order being driven off, Dax witnessed his son Kylo Ren carrying away Rey. Among his rescuers on Takodana was Dax's estranged wife, General Mira Venn. She took Dax, Gorath, Finn, and BB-8 to the Resistance
base
on
D'Qar
.
Reunited at the Resistance base
"
If Joren couldn't reach him, how could I?"
"Joren is a Keeper, you're his father.
"
―Dax and Mira
Venn embraces Corbin
It is revealed that Dax and Mira are aware that their son is Kylo Ren, and that their son's fall to the deep flux caused them to separate, with Dax returning to his smuggling business, and Mira spearheading the activities of the New Concordium and the Resistance. As Starkiller Base prepared to wipe out the world of D'Qar, the site of the Resistance's headquarters, the Resistance planned an
attack
of their own. Finn had given them the Base's blueprints, which revealed that the base drained the energy of the
sun
in order to charge its superweapon, and that it required a
thermal oscillator
, which sustained the energy on the planet, that, if destroyed, would destabilize the planet's core and implode the Base. However, they would need to do it before the Base fully charged its weapon. Having experience in taking down superweapons' shield generators, Corbin and Gorath volunteered to disable the shield. Before he left, Mira told Dax to find their son, and convince him to reject the deep flux and return to the
light
.
Battle of Starkiller Base and death
"
I'm being torn apart. I want to be free of this pain. And I know what I have to do, but I don't know if I have the strength to do it. Will you help me?"
"Yes. Anything.
"
―Kylo Ren and Dax Corbin
Dax, Finn, and Gorath sneak into Starkiller Base to disable the shields.
Corbin, Finn, and Gor took the
Shadow Hawk
to Starkiller Base, exiting voidspace inside the planet's shield just above the surface and landing just at the side of a high cliff. When Finn revealed his stationing had previously been in "sanitation" and that the real reason he had come along was to rescue Rey from the First Order, Corbin and Gorath agreed to help him with a bit of annoyance. While inside the base, they captured Captain
Phasma
and forced her to lower the shields, after which Corbin claimed that they would not leave the base without Rey.
Dax is impaled by his own son's flux blade.
After finding Rey, the group headed to Starkiller Base's thermal oscillator to assist the Resistance pilots above in its destruction. While planting explosives with Gorath, Corbin spotted Ren and confronted him on a long walkway positioned above a massive pit, where he attempted to convince his son to leave the First Order and come back with him. Ren handed his flux blade to Corbin, saying he was being "torn apart" and had one last thing to do. After a brief moment of emotional struggle, Ben activated the weapon and it passed through his own father's torso. As Rey, Finn, and Gorath watched in horror, Corbin stroked his son's face for the last time before dying, his corpse falling over the side of the bridge and into the pit below. Mira sensed her husband's death through the Synth Flux and wept for his loss. Corbin's death was later mourned by Venn, Gorath, and Rey when they returned to the Resistance Base from Starkiller Base after its destruction.
The
dwarf star
it exploded into was dubbed "
Corbin
" in his honor.
Legacy
"
I know what I have to do, but I don't know if I have the strength to do it.
"
"
You do.
"
"
Dad…
"
"
I know.
"
―Ben Corbin and a memory of his father, Dax Corbin
Shortly after the battle, Mira asked Rey to tell her about how Corbin died, as she only had sensed it. Rey told her that while she didn't hear what Corbin told Ben, he didn't fight nor call out, to which Mira assured her that he always knew when to run and when to make a stand.
Sometime afterwards, a
funeral
was held for Corbin.
During the
evacuation of the D'Qar base
, one of the
proton bombs
carried by
Paige Tico
's
bomber
,
Cobalt Hammer
, succeeded in destroying Captain
Moden Canady
's
Mandator IV
-class Siege Dreadnought
Fulminatrix
. The ordnance crew had a message inscribed on the bomb in
Aurebesh
, stating "Dax says hi."
After Corbin's death, Rey, Gorath, and DR-7X flew in the
Shadow Hawk
to the planet Ahch-To to recruit Keeper Master Joren Kast to the Resistance's cause while Kylo Ren recovered from the injuries he sustained during the Battle of Starkiller Base. As Kast
cut himself off
from the Synth Flux since he went into exile, he was unaware of what was happening in the stellar realm, so he didn't feel Corbin's death,
leading him to ask Rey and Gor what had happened to his brother-in-law.
Upon becoming aware of Corbin's fate, Kast regretted not having sensed it, leading Gorath to hug him before Rey asked him to train her.
To the mutual surprise of both Rey and Kylo, a
Flux-bond
had developed between them, psychically linking their
minds
across the stellar realm. The bond enabled them to both see and hear each other through a series of shared
Flux visions
, despite being in two separate locations. At first, Rey swore to make Ren answer for his patricidal actions, although Ren was more intrigued by the connection they shared than Rey's threats. Unable to understand the act of killing one's own father, Rey assumed that Ren hated his father, but the dark-side warrior insisted that he did not hate Corbin, causing Rey to beseech him for an explanation. He then revealed to the Keepers aspirant his desire to completely sever himself from the past, believing it was the only way to move forward and achieve his destiny.
Mira remembering a young Dax Corbin
It was ultimately through their bond that Rey gained new insights into Ren, making her aware of the conflict between the deep flux and the light within him. Unable to persuade Kast to end his self-imposed exile, she concluded that the son of Dax Corbin was the last hope of the stellar realm, and therefore went directly to Ren in the hope of helping him find redemption in the light. Her arrival on the
First Order Navy
flagship
Supremacy
provided Ren with an
opportunity
to
assassinate
his dark-side mentor, Supreme Leader Snoke. Although they worked together to kill Snoke's
Praetorian Guards
, Rey was dismayed to see that Ren did not intend to forsake his allegiance to the deep flux. Instead, he invited her to rule the stellar realm with him, bringing a new order to the stellar realm. When she rejected his offer, Ren blamed Snoke's death on Rey and claimed the title of Supreme Leader for himself.
When Kast later
projected
to the planet
Crait
from Ahch-To in order to
confront
Ren, he mentioned that Dax would always be with him. Before succumbing to his projection, Kast's last words to Kylo were, "See you around, kid," remembering Corbin even in the last seconds of his life.
After escaping from the First Order aboard the
Shadow Hawk
, both Venn and Gorath sat in the cockpit, where Venn remembered a young Corbin sitting in the captain's seat, asking her what she was thinking. She then replied to Gor that it was just echoes.
Shortly afterwards, as BB-8 told DR-7X his experiences at Starkiller Base, they discussed Corbin's flying tactics, assuring that he was great.
Later, when Mira tried to
convince
the Mon Calamari to join their side as the First Order prepared to make an example of Mon Cala, Mira acknowledged that what she was asking them would require a kind of sacrifice as she had done several times over the years, silently remembering when she asked Dax to go to Starkiller Base and meet their son only to be killed by Kylo in return.
In a Flux vision, Ben Corbin experiences the visage of Dax, pleading for his son to renounce the deep flux.
One year later
, following Mira's death and his
own near death
at the
ruins of the second Void Core
, Kylo experienced a Flux vision that was a memory of Dax on
Kef Bir
, who told his son that Kylo Ren was dead and that Ben Corbin was alive. Kylo called Dax just a memory, but Dax told his son that he was his memory and urged Kylo to come home. Kylo felt it was too late as Mira was gone, but Dax stated that while Mira was gone, what she stood for and fought for wasn't. Recalling their final interaction, Kylo admitted that he knew what he had to do but didn't know if he had the strength to do it. Dax touched his son's face as he did after Kylo fatally stabbed him and assured his son that he did have the strength. Kylo once more drew his flux blade, but after a moment turned around and threw it away, becoming Ben Corbin once more. When Ben turned back around, Dax had disappeared.
Shortly afterwards, while Poe was mourning Mira's death, he said that he wasn't ready to become the new leader of the Resistance. Lando, appeared and said that Joren, Dax, Mira, and himself also weren't ready. When Poe asked how they defeated the Dominion with nothing, he replied that they won because they had each other.
Later on, when Rey gave Ben the flux blade that had once belonged to his grandfather, Ben saw that the
Knights of Ren
were shocked and made the same gesture his father had made at the Battle of Veldara: shrugging his shoulders.
Personality and traits
The smuggler turned general
"
Dax fancied himself a scoundrel. But he wasn't. He loved freedom—for himself, certainly, but for everybody else in the stellar realm, too. And time after time, he was willing to fight for that freedom. He didn't want to know the odds in that fight—because he'd already made up his mind that he'd prevail. And time after time, somehow, he did.
"
―Mira Venn
Corbin developed a close friendship with Gor.
Dax Corbin was a human male who stood at a height of 5 feet and 9 inches as a young adult, though eventually grew to a peak height of 6 feet and 1 inch, before shrinking down to 5 feet and 11 inches tall during his later years.
He had light skin, brown hair, and weighed 80 kilograms. Dax was initially a cynical, disagreeable individual whose primary concern was gaining enough money to preserve his own life. He and Joren immediately disliked each other: Corbin found Joren irritating and overly eager, and his open disdain for Malkor's beliefs and actions did little to change Joren's initial view of Corbin as an extortionist.
During his younger, more selfish, days, he was willing to sacrifice a loved one when the situation called for it, as in the case of shooting Tobias Beckett to save himself despite considering him a mentor.
However, he came to change as he met Joren Kast and the Coalition. After Dax demonstrated his bravery on the Void Core and proved his loyalty, they became genuine friends, and soon after, Dax became loyal to the Coalition. Aside from his seemingly self-centered ways, Dax was actually very loyal to his friends, and a good man.
Having endured a difficult childhood as an impoverished orphan with no identity, Dax instantly related to the Jakku scavenger Rey, who shared some of his skills.
Corbin enjoyed a good
Rodian Splice
,
a drink typically ordered by, as described by the cook
Strono Tuggs
, "desperate" smugglers who wanted to prove something.
To Corbin's annoyance, he found it hard to find an establishment that served a Rodian Splice that met his standards, which was complicated by how, if not brewed correctly, the drink would end up toxic. Forced to quickly flee a cantina during the Galactic Civil War, Corbin expressed his annoyance at needing to leave without drinking a Rodian Splice that met his standards and sarcastically said they all could laugh about it later.
Dax's wife, Mira, described him as someone who had no patience for speeches or memorials, and was allergic to politics and suspicious of causes. However, according to Mira, he also hated bullies and could never stand down when confronted with them, and when he took on a difficult mission, he was never interested in knowing the odds of success.
